| 声明 | 行为与约束 |
| --- | --- |
| <a id="api-feae261bf2e2ac83"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadMapping(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34258) | Get the current mapping of a gamepad. Details about mappings are discussed with SDL_AddGamepadMapping(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AddGamepadMapping @sa SDL_GetGamepadMappingForID @sa SDL_GetGamepadMappingForGUID @sa SDL_SetGamepadMapping<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad you want to get the current mapping for.<br>返回：a string that has the gamepad's mapping or NULL if no mapping is available; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-e8b5c71868bdfe2a"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadMappingForGuid(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGUID* guid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27822) | Get the gamepad mapping string for a given GUID. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickGUIDForID @sa SDL_GetJoystickGUID<br>`guid`：a structure containing the GUID for which a mapping is desired.<br>返回：a mapping string or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-b1ba7f1ea49aea3a"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadMappingForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51554) | Get the mapping of a gamepad. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepads @sa SDL_GetGamepadMapping<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the mapping string. Returns NULL if no mapping is available. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-36577ac9cae91455"></a>[`public sbyte** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadMappings(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22641) | Get the current gamepad mappings. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of mappings returned, can be NULL.<br>返回：an array of the mapping strings, NULL-terminated, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This is a single allocation that should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-b1437d35560dc695"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadName(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50902) | Get the implementation-dependent name for an opened gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadNameForID<br>`gamepad`：a gamepad identifier previously returned by SDL_OpenGamepad().<br>返回：the implementation dependent name for the gamepad, or NULL if there is no name or the identifier passed is invalid. |
| <a id="api-2adf71c7ec39a6de"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadNameForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25752) | Get the implementation dependent name of a gamepad. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadName @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the name of the selected gamepad. If no name can be found, this function returns NULL; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8707453915bd729a"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadPath(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47875) | Get the implementation-dependent path for an opened gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadPathForID<br>`gamepad`：a gamepad identifier previously returned by SDL_OpenGamepad().<br>返回：the implementation dependent path for the gamepad, or NULL if there is no path or the identifier passed is invalid. |
| <a id="api-52ffc167a78a7f2a"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadPathForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22839) | Get the implementation dependent path of a gamepad. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadPath @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the path of the selected gamepad. If no path can be found, this function returns NULL; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-546a11fc94e44607"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadPlayerIndex(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34151) | Get the player index of an opened gamepad. For XInput gamepads this returns the XInput user index. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetGamepadPlayerIndex<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad object to query.<br>返回：the player index for gamepad, or -1 if it's not available. |
| <a id="api-3c3db4f074ba7b3b"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadPlayerIndexForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33283) | Get the player index of a gamepad. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadPlayerIndex @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the player index of a gamepad, or -1 if it's not available. |
| <a id="api-3235edecc66cc709"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPowerState Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadPowerInfo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, int* percent)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34941) | Get the battery state of a gamepad. You should never take a battery status as absolute truth. Batteries (especially failing batteries) are delicate hardware, and the values reported here are best estimates based on what that hardware reports. It's not uncommon for older batteries to lose stored power much faster than it reports, or completely drain when reporting it has 20 percent left, etc. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad object to query.<br>`percent`：a pointer filled in with the percentage of battery life left, between 0 and 100, or NULL to ignore. This will be filled in with -1 we can't determine a value or there is no battery.<br>返回：the current battery state. |
| <a id="api-41fa8922bc63bca6"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadProduct(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34867) | Get the USB product ID of an opened gamepad, if available. If the product ID isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadProductForID<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad object to query.<br>返回：the USB product ID, or zero if unavailable. |
| <a id="api-c601301e265d43c0"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadProductForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19041) | Get the USB product ID of a gamepad, if available. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. If the product ID isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadProduct @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the USB product ID of the selected gamepad. If called on an invalid index, this function returns zero. |
| <a id="api-4d710010d191c81d"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadProductVersion(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42492) | Get the product version of an opened gamepad, if available. If the product version isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadProductVersionForID<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad object to query.<br>返回：the USB product version, or zero if unavailable. |
| <a id="api-2f7e7b9b07736ad5"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadProductVersionForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42647) | Get the product version of a gamepad, if available. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. If the product version isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadProductVersion @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the product version of the selected gamepad. If called on an invalid index, this function returns zero. |
| <a id="api-9caee8925f6c2964"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32255) | Get the properties associated with an opened gamepad. These properties are shared with the underlying joystick object. The following read-only properties are provided by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_GAMEPAD_CAP_MONO_LED_BOOLEAN`: true if this gamepad has an LED that has adjustable brightness - `SDL_PROP_GAMEPAD_CAP_RGB_LED_BOOLEAN`: true if this gamepad has an LED that has adjustable color - `SDL_PROP_GAMEPAD_CAP_PLAYER_LED_BOOLEAN`: true if this gamepad has a player LED - `SDL_PROP_GAMEPAD_CAP_RUMBLE_BOOLEAN`: true if this gamepad has left/right rumble - `SDL_PROP_GAMEPAD_CAP_TRIGGER_RUMBLE_BOOLEAN`: true if this gamepad has simple trigger rumble @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：a gamepad identifier previously returned by SDL_OpenGamepad().<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5616d01553fb4218"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadSensorData(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensorType type, float* data, int num_values)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47997) | Get the current state of a gamepad sensor. The number of values and interpretation of the data is sensor dependent. See the remarks in SDL_SensorType for details for each type of sensor. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad to query.<br>`type`：the type of sensor to query.<br>`data`：a pointer filled with the current sensor state.<br>`num_values`：the number of values to write to data.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-fa1e343126127f1e"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadSensorDataRate(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensorType type)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26757) | Get the data rate (number of events per second) of a gamepad sensor. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad to query.<br>`type`：the type of sensor to query.<br>返回：the data rate, or 0.0f if the data rate is not available. |

The string returned is of the format used by SDL_Gamepad mapping strings. |

The string returned is of the format used by SDL_Gamepad mapping strings. |

The string returned is of the format used by SDL_Gamepad mapping strings. |
| <a id="api-66d424d59dad25b9"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadTouchpadFinger(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, int touchpad, int finger, byte* down, float* x, float* y, float* pressure)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43356) | Get the current state of a finger on a touchpad on a gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumGamepadTouchpadFingers<br>`gamepad`：a gamepad.<br>`touchpad`：a touchpad.<br>`finger`：a finger.<br>`down`：a pointer filled with true if the finger is down, false otherwise, may be NULL.<br>`x`：a pointer filled with the x position, normalized 0 to 1, with the origin in the upper left, may be NULL.<br>`y`：a pointer filled with the y position, normalized 0 to 1, with the origin in the upper left, may be NULL.<br>`pressure`：a pointer filled with pressure value, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e0aa6bd5f60696c6"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadType(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21429) | Get the type of an opened gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadTypeForID<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad object to query.<br>返回：the gamepad type, or SDL_GAMEPAD_TYPE_UNKNOWN if it's not available. |
| <a id="api-c07873f1d9ceabff"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadTypeForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50700) | Get the type of a gamepad. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadType @sa SDL_GetGamepads @sa SDL_GetRealGamepadTypeForID<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the gamepad type. |
| <a id="api-13bb93dd2bed5038"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadTypeFromString(sbyte* str)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38109) | Convert a string into SDL_GamepadType enum. This function is called internally to translate SDL_Gamepad mapping strings for the underlying joystick device into the consistent SDL_Gamepad mapping. You do not normally need to call this function unless you are parsing SDL_Gamepad mappings in your own code. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadStringForType<br>`str`：string representing a SDL_GamepadType type.<br>返回：the SDL_GamepadType enum corresponding to the input string, or `SDL_GAMEPAD_TYPE_UNKNOWN` if no match was found. |
| <a id="api-94824ae8035e6828"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadVendor(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24440) | Get the USB vendor ID of an opened gamepad, if available. If the vendor ID isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadVendorForID<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad object to query.<br>返回：the USB vendor ID, or zero if unavailable. |
| <a id="api-5a42a84cde898c6f"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadVendorForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16980) | Get the USB vendor ID of a gamepad, if available. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. If the vendor ID isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadVendor @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the USB vendor ID of the selected gamepad. If called on an invalid index, this function returns zero. |
| <a id="api-9d712392c739e297"></a>[`public uint* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepads(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38133) | Get a list of currently connected gamepads. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasGamepad @sa SDL_OpenGamepad<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of gamepads returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a 0 terminated array of joystick instance IDs or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-092ddc8c60886004"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGlobalMouseState(float* x, float* y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23979) | Query the platform for the asynchronous mouse button state and the desktop-relative platform-cursor position. This function immediately queries the platform for the most recent asynchronous state, more costly than retrieving SDL's cached state in SDL_GetMouseState(). Passing non-NULL pointers to `x` or `y` will write the destination with respective x or y coordinates relative to the desktop. In Relative Mode, the platform-cursor's position usually contradicts the SDL-cursor's position as manually calculated from SDL_GetMouseState() and SDL_GetWindowPosition. This function can be useful if you need to track the mouse outside of a specific window and SDL_CaptureMouse() doesn't fit your needs. For example, it could be useful if you need to track the mouse while dragging a window, where coordinates relative to a window might not be in sync at all times. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CaptureMouse @sa SDL_GetMouseState @sa SDL_GetGlobalMouseState<br>`x`：a pointer to receive the platform-cursor's x-position from the desktop's top left corner, can be NULL if unused.<br>`y`：a pointer to receive the platform-cursor's y-position from the desktop's top left corner, can be NULL if unused.<br>返回：a 32-bit bitmask of the button state that can be bitwise-compared against the SDL_BUTTON_MASK(X) macro. |
| <a id="api-c114a4c14b228026"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGlobalProperties()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23859) | Get the global SDL properties. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-64cb13d7f265b9e6"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGpuDeviceDriver(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28654) | Returns the name of the backend used to create this GPU context. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`device`：a GPU context to query.<br>返回：the name of the device's driver, or NULL on error. |
| <a id="api-e80d73941901f7b9"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGpuDeviceProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22618) | Get the properties associated with a GPU device. All properties are optional and may differ between GPU backends and SDL versions. The following properties are provided by SDL: `SDL_PROP_GPU_DEVICE_NAME_STRING`: Contains the name of the underlying device as reported by the system driver. This string has no standardized format, is highly inconsistent between hardware devices and drivers, and is able to change at any time. Do not attempt to parse this string as it is bound to fail at some point in the future when system drivers are updated, new hardware devices are introduced, or when SDL adds new GPU backends or modifies existing ones. Strings that have been found in the wild include: - GTX 970 - GeForce GTX 970 - NVIDIA GeForce GTX 970 - Microsoft Direct3D12 (NVIDIA GeForce GTX 970) - NVIDIA Graphics Device - GeForce GPU - P106-100 - AMD 15D8:C9 - AMD Custom GPU 0405 - AMD Radeon (TM) Graphics - ASUS Radeon RX 470 Series - Intel(R) Arc(tm) A380 Graphics (DG2) - Virtio-GPU Venus (NVIDIA TITAN V) - SwiftShader Device (LLVM 16.0.0) - llvmpipe (LLVM 15.0.4, 256 bits) - Microsoft Basic Render Driver - unknown device The above list shows that the same device can have different formats, the vendor name may or may not appear in the string, the included vendor name may not be the vendor of the chipset on the device, some manufacturers include pseudo-legal marks while others don't, some devices may not use a marketing name in the string, the device string may be wrapped by the name of a translation interface, the device may be emulated in software, or the string may contain generic text that does not identify the device at all. `SDL_PROP_GPU_DEVICE_DRIVER_NAME_STRING`: Contains the self-reported name of the underlying system driver. Strings that have been found in the wild include: - Intel Corporation - Intel open-source Mesa driver - Qualcomm Technologies Inc. Adreno Vulkan Driver - MoltenVK - Mali-G715 - venus `SDL_PROP_GPU_DEVICE_DRIVER_VERSION_STRING`: Contains the self-reported version of the underlying system driver. This is a relatively short version string in an unspecified format. If SDL_PROP_GPU_DEVICE_DRIVER_INFO_STRING is available then that property should be preferred over this one as it may contain additional information that is useful for identifying the exact driver version used. Strings that have been found in the wild include: - 53.0.0 - 0.405.2463 - 32.0.15.6614 `SDL_PROP_GPU_DEVICE_DRIVER_INFO_STRING`: Contains the detailed version information of the underlying system driver as reported by the driver. This is an arbitrary string with no standardized format and it may contain newlines. This property should be preferred over SDL_PROP_GPU_DEVICE_DRIVER_VERSION_STRING if it is available as it usually contains the same information but in a format that is easier to read. Strings that have been found in the wild include: - 101.6559 - 1.2.11 - Mesa 21.2.2 (LLVM 12.0.1) - Mesa 22.2.0-devel (git-f226222 2022-04-14 impish-oibaf-ppa) - v1.r53p0-00eac0.824c4f31403fb1fbf8ee1042422c2129 This string has also been observed to be a multiline string (which has a trailing newline): ``` Driver Build: 85da404, I46ff5fc46f, 1606794520 Date: 11/30/20 Compiler Version: EV031.31.04.01 Driver Branch: promo490_3_Google ``` @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`device`：a GPU context to query.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-711f917bf9d5deae"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGpuDriver(int index)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25957) | Get the name of a built in GPU driver. The GPU drivers are presented in the order in which they are normally checked during initialization. The names of drivers are all simple, low-ASCII identifiers, like "vulkan", "metal" or "direct3d12". These never have Unicode characters, and are not meant to be proper names. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumGPUDrivers<br>`index`：the index of a GPU driver.<br>返回：the name of the GPU driver with the given **index**. |
| <a id="api-0eb03332662601f4"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGpuRendererDevice(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19240) | Return the GPU device used by a renderer. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>返回：the GPU device used by the renderer, or NULL if the renderer is not a GPU renderer; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-41a2c8bf1371de13"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGpuShaderFormats(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18952) | Returns the supported shader formats for this GPU context. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`device`：a GPU context to query.<br>返回：a bitflag indicating which shader formats the driver is able to consume. |
| <a id="api-9c306ef2582267ef"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureFormat Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGpuSwapchainTextureFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32046) | Obtains the texture format of the swapchain for the given window. Note that this format can change if the swapchain parameters change. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>`window`：an SDL_Window that has been claimed.<br>返回：the texture format of the swapchain. |
| <a id="api-127aaf46c927bfe0"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureFormat Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGpuTextureFormatFromPixelFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28113) | Get the GPU texture format corresponding to an SDL pixel format. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`format`：a pixel format.<br>返回：the corresponding GPU texture format, or SDL_GPU_TEXTUREFORMAT_INVALID if there is no corresponding GPU texture format. |
| <a id="api-5399e0291fbfc553"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGrabbedWindow()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32273) | Get the window that currently has an input grab enabled. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowMouseGrab @sa SDL_SetWindowKeyboardGrab<br>返回：the window if input is grabbed or NULL otherwise. |
| <a id="api-683b83cca5312fe2"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetHapticEffectStatus(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic, int effect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52214) | Get the status of the current effect on the specified haptic device. Device must support the SDL_HAPTIC_STATUS feature. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetHapticFeatures<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to query for the effect status on.<br>`effect`：the ID of the haptic effect to query its status.<br>返回：true if it is playing, false if it isn't playing or haptic status isn't supported. |
| <a id="api-edb8eb9da133eca3"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetHapticFeatures(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49863) | Get the haptic device's supported features in bitwise manner. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HapticEffectSupported @sa SDL_GetMaxHapticEffects<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to query.<br>返回：a list of supported haptic features in bitwise manner (OR'd), or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b8f3fe3fed949e02"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetHapticFromId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49771) | Get the SDL_Haptic associated with an instance ID, if it has been opened. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the instance ID to get the SDL_Haptic for.<br>返回：an SDL_Haptic on success or NULL on failure or if it hasn't been opened yet; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-dacc17cdd5dd4fee"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetHapticId(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37220) | Get the instance ID of an opened haptic device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to query.<br>返回：the instance ID of the specified haptic device on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4fef86a052e182a5"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetHapticName(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17540) | Get the implementation dependent name of a haptic device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetHapticNameForID<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic obtained from SDL_OpenJoystick().<br>返回：the name of the selected haptic device. If no name can be found, this function returns NULL; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-721a72a431b6fae2"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetHapticNameForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23695) | Get the implementation dependent name of a haptic device. This can be called before any haptic devices are opened. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetHapticName @sa SDL_OpenHaptic<br>`instance_id`：the haptic device instance ID.<br>返回：the name of the selected haptic device. If no name can be found, this function returns NULL; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3a04ebaf19c91a4f"></a>[`public uint* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetHaptics(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47555) | Get a list of currently connected haptic devices. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenHaptic<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of haptic devices returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a 0 terminated array of haptic device instance IDs or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-f4f9953aab09ce7f"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetHint(sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26158) | Get the value of a hint. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetHint @sa SDL_SetHintWithPriority<br>`name`：the hint to query.<br>返回：the string value of a hint or NULL if the hint isn't set. |
| <a id="api-ed5def6c916094cf"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetHintBoolean(sbyte* name, byte default_value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52159) | Get the boolean value of a hint variable. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetHint @sa SDL_SetHint<br>`name`：the name of the hint to get the boolean value from.<br>`default_value`：the value to return if the hint does not exist.<br>返回：the boolean value of a hint or the provided default value if the hint does not exist. |
| <a id="api-74032d6ac675c3cc"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetIoProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* context)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24386) | Get the properties associated with an SDL_IOStream. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`context`：a pointer to an SDL_IOStream structure.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f2b81d1033da598c"></a>[`public long Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetIoSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* context)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27124) | Use this function to get the size of the data stream in an SDL_IOStream. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`context`：the SDL_IOStream to get the size of the data stream from.<br>返回：the size of the data stream in the SDL_IOStream on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ea1c3e46c0dba474"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStatus Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetIoStatus(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* context)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48203) | Query the stream status of an SDL_IOStream. This information can be useful to decide if a short read or write was due to an error, an EOF, or a non-blocking operation that isn't yet ready to complete. An SDL_IOStream's status is only expected to change after a SDL_ReadIO or SDL_WriteIO call; don't expect it to change if you just call this query function in a tight loop. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`context`：the SDL_IOStream to query.<br>返回：an SDL_IOStatus enum with the current state. |
| <a id="api-19c928c5d66db18f"></a>[`public short Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickAxis(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, int axis)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37174) | Get the current state of an axis control on a joystick. SDL makes no promises about what part of the joystick any given axis refers to. Your game should have some sort of configuration UI to let users specify what each axis should be bound to. Alternately, SDL's higher-level Game Controller API makes a great effort to apply order to this lower-level interface, so you know that a specific axis is the "left thumb stick," etc. The value returned by SDL_GetJoystickAxis() is a signed integer (-32768 to 32767) representing the current position of the axis. It may be necessary to impose certain tolerances on these values to account for jitter. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickAxes<br>`joystick`：an SDL_Joystick structure containing joystick information.<br>`axis`：the axis to query; the axis indices start at index 0.<br>返回：a 16-bit signed integer representing the current position of the axis or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-954fbeda9f94e698"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickAxisInitialState(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, int axis, short* state)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26006) | Get the initial state of an axis control on a joystick. The state is a value ranging from -32768 to 32767. The axis indices start at index 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`joystick`：an SDL_Joystick structure containing joystick information.<br>`axis`：the axis to query; the axis indices start at index 0.<br>`state`：upon return, the initial value is supplied here.<br>返回：true if this axis has any initial value, or false if not. |
| <a id="api-63738c77380ba87e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickBall(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, int ball, int* dx, int* dy)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18736) | Get the ball axis change since the last poll. Trackballs can only return relative motion since the last call to SDL_GetJoystickBall(), these motion deltas are placed into `dx` and `dy`. Most joysticks do not have trackballs. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickBalls<br>`joystick`：the SDL_Joystick to query.<br>`ball`：the ball index to query; ball indices start at index 0.<br>`dx`：stores the difference in the x axis position since the last poll.<br>`dy`：stores the difference in the y axis position since the last poll.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ee95eb866bf8c4da"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickButton(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, int button)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18354) | Get the current state of a button on a joystick. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickButtons<br>`joystick`：an SDL_Joystick structure containing joystick information.<br>`button`：the button index to get the state from; indices start at index 0.<br>返回：true if the button is pressed, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-63fb2c99c0b68a66"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystickConnectionState Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickConnectionState(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38462) | Get the connection state of a joystick. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`joystick`：the joystick to query.<br>返回：the connection state on success or `SDL_JOYSTICK_CONNECTION_INVALID` on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5dd2efea709f0390"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickFirmwareVersion(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36018) | Get the firmware version of an opened joystick, if available. If the firmware version isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`joystick`：the SDL_Joystick obtained from SDL_OpenJoystick().<br>返回：the firmware version of the selected joystick, or 0 if unavailable. |
| <a id="api-afee3b4bc0c74a94"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickFromId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34961) | Get the SDL_Joystick associated with an instance ID, if it has been opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the instance ID to get the SDL_Joystick for.<br>返回：an SDL_Joystick on success or NULL on failure or if it hasn't been opened yet; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-21ad24e570dcb2f7"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickFromPlayerIndex(int player_index)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28589) | Get the SDL_Joystick associated with a player index. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickPlayerIndex @sa SDL_SetJoystickPlayerIndex<br>`player_index`：the player index to get the SDL_Joystick for.<br>返回：an SDL_Joystick on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5d179f5c2b955e5d"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickGuid(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGUID* bgcs_result)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37116) | Get the implementation-dependent GUID for the joystick. This function requires an open joystick. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickGUIDForID @sa SDL_GUIDToString<br>`joystick`：the SDL_Joystick obtained from SDL_OpenJoystick().<br>`bgcs_result`：Native parameter.<br>约束：Native result: the GUID of the given joystick. If called on an invalid index, this function returns a zero GUID; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9b700f492ca66b05"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickGuidForId(uint instance_id, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGUID* bgcs_result)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30893) | Get the implementation-dependent GUID of a joystick. This can be called before any joysticks are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickGUID @sa SDL_GUIDToString<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>`bgcs_result`：Native parameter.<br>约束：Native result: the GUID of the selected joystick. If called with an invalid instance_id, this function returns a zero GUID. |
| <a id="api-6da7f761d186ee1a"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickGuidInfo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGUID* guid, ushort* vendor, ushort* product, ushort* version, ushort* crc16)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23466) | Get the device information encoded in a SDL_GUID structure. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickGUIDForID<br>`guid`：the SDL_GUID you wish to get info about.<br>`vendor`：a pointer filled in with the device VID, or 0 if not available.<br>`product`：a pointer filled in with the device PID, or 0 if not available.<br>`version`：a pointer filled in with the device version, or 0 if not available.<br>`crc16`：a pointer filled in with a CRC used to distinguish different products with the same VID/PID, or 0 if not available. |
| <a id="api-0bad8390ddc3ac50"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickHat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, int hat)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33125) | Get the current state of a POV hat on a joystick. The returned value will be one of the `SDL_HAT_*` values. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickHats<br>`joystick`：an SDL_Joystick structure containing joystick information.<br>`hat`：the hat index to get the state from; indices start at index 0.<br>返回：the current hat position. |

If no path can be found, this function returns NULL; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9c5ad1caf1267675"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickPlayerIndex(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32887) | Get the player index of an opened joystick. For XInput controllers this returns the XInput user index. Many joysticks will not be able to supply this information. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetJoystickPlayerIndex<br>`joystick`：the SDL_Joystick obtained from SDL_OpenJoystick().<br>返回：the player index, or -1 if it's not available. |

This can be called before any joysticks are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickPlayerIndex @sa SDL_GetJoysticks<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the player index of a joystick, or -1 if it's not available. |
| <a id="api-708e774d6c7b0e41"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPowerState Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickPowerInfo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, int* percent)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27337) | Get the battery state of a joystick. You should never take a battery status as absolute truth. Batteries (especially failing batteries) are delicate hardware, and the values reported here are best estimates based on what that hardware reports. It's not uncommon for older batteries to lose stored power much faster than it reports, or completely drain when reporting it has 20 percent left, etc. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`joystick`：the joystick to query.<br>`percent`：a pointer filled in with the percentage of battery life left, between 0 and 100, or NULL to ignore. This will be filled in with -1 we can't determine a value or there is no battery.<br>返回：the current battery state or `SDL_POWERSTATE_ERROR` on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-93f4412172a87062"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickProduct(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29288) | Get the USB product ID of an opened joystick, if available. If the product ID isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickProductForID<br>`joystick`：the SDL_Joystick obtained from SDL_OpenJoystick().<br>返回：the USB product ID of the selected joystick, or 0 if unavailable. |

This can be called before any joysticks are opened. If called with an invalid instance_id, this function returns 0. |

If called with an invalid instance_id, this function returns 0. |
| <a id="api-ebe84283d2290147"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetJoystickProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19721) | Get the properties associated with a joystick. The following read-only properties are provided by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_JOYSTICK_CAP_MONO_LED_BOOLEAN`: true if this joystick has an LED that has adjustable brightness - `SDL_PROP_JOYSTICK_CAP_RGB_LED_BOOLEAN`: true if this joystick has an LED that has adjustable color - `SDL_PROP_JOYSTICK_CAP_PLAYER_LED_BOOLEAN`: true if this joystick has a player LED - `SDL_PROP_JOYSTICK_CAP_RUMBLE_BOOLEAN`: true if this joystick has left/right rumble - `SDL_PROP_JOYSTICK_CAP_TRIGGER_RUMBLE_BOOLEAN`: true if this joystick has simple trigger rumble @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`joystick`：the SDL_Joystick obtained from SDL_OpenJoystick().<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |

Returns the serial number of the joystick, or NULL if it is not available. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`joystick`：the SDL_Joystick obtained from SDL_OpenJoystick().<br>返回：the serial number of the selected joystick, or NULL if unavailable. |

This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-b086200f0affe43c"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetKeyFromName(sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26513) | Get a key code from a human-readable name. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyFromScancode @sa SDL_GetKeyName @sa SDL_GetScancodeFromName<br>`name`：the human-readable key name.<br>返回：key code, or `SDLK_UNKNOWN` if the name wasn't recognized; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-cee17387f1b9d0fe"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetKeyFromScancode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScancode scancode, ushort modstate, byte key_event)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23189) | Get the key code corresponding to the given scancode according to the current keyboard layout. If you want to get the keycode as it would be delivered in key events, including options specified in SDL_HINT_KEYCODE_OPTIONS, then you should pass `key_event` as true. Otherwise this function simply translates the scancode based on the given modifier state. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyName @sa SDL_GetScancodeFromKey<br>`scancode`：the desired SDL_Scancode to query.<br>`modstate`：the modifier state to use when translating the scancode to a keycode.<br>`key_event`：true if the keycode will be used in key events.<br>返回：the SDL_Keycode that corresponds to the given SDL_Scancode. |
| <a id="api-7e448e8210276531"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetKeyName(uint key)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39946) | Get a human-readable name for a key. If the key doesn't have a name, this function returns an empty string (""). Letters will be presented in their uppercase form, if applicable. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyFromName @sa SDL_GetKeyFromScancode @sa SDL_GetScancodeFromKey<br>`key`：the desired SDL_Keycode to query.<br>返回：a UTF-8 encoded string of the key name. |
| <a id="api-b60ee9bd698fef8b"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetKeyboardFocus()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41329) | Query the window which currently has keyboard focus. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the window with keyboard focus. |
| <a id="api-3cc21ca61eebd974"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetKeyboardNameForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26292) | Get the name of a keyboard. This function returns "" if the keyboard doesn't have a name. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyboards<br>`instance_id`：the keyboard instance ID.<br>返回：the name of the selected keyboard or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-da295e85bcbbb1c6"></a>[`public byte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetKeyboardState(int* numkeys)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45879) | Get a snapshot of the current state of the keyboard. The pointer returned is a pointer to an internal SDL array. It will be valid for the whole lifetime of the application and should not be freed by the caller. A array element with a value of true means that the key is pressed and a value of false means that it is not. Indexes into this array are obtained by using SDL_Scancode values. Use SDL_PumpEvents() to update the state array. This function gives you the current state after all events have been processed, so if a key or button has been pressed and released before you process events, then the pressed state will never show up in the SDL_GetKeyboardState() calls. Note: This function doesn't take into account whether shift has been pressed or not. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PumpEvents @sa SDL_ResetKeyboard<br>`numkeys`：if non-NULL, receives the length of the returned array.<br>返回：a pointer to an array of key states. |
| <a id="api-da17a4c045f32ed2"></a>[`public uint* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetKeyboards(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48025) | Get a list of currently connected keyboards. Note that this will include any device or virtual driver that includes keyboard functionality, including some mice, KVM switches, motherboard power buttons, etc. You should wait for input from a device before you consider it actively in use. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyboardNameForID @sa SDL_HasKeyboard<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of keyboards returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a 0 terminated array of keyboards instance IDs or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-5e184a2467705c6e"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetLogOutputFunction(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, int, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLLogPriority, sbyte*, void>* callback, void** userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39990) | Get the current log output function. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDefaultLogOutputFunction @sa SDL_SetLogOutputFunction<br>`callback`：an SDL_LogOutputFunction filled in with the current log callback.<br>`userdata`：a pointer filled in with the pointer that is passed to `callback`. |
| <a id="api-705074af2c83d4e8"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLLogPriority Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetLogPriority(int category)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20866) | Get the priority of a particular log category. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetLogPriority<br>`category`：the category to query.<br>返回：the SDL_LogPriority for the requested category. |
| <a id="api-7b64284be8e88cfa"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetMasksForPixelFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat format, int* bpp, uint* Rmask, uint* Gmask, uint* Bmask, uint* Amask)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49333) | Convert one of the enumerated pixel formats to a bpp value and RGBA masks. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPixelFormatForMasks<br>`format`：one of the SDL_PixelFormat values.<br>`bpp`：a bits per pixel value; usually 15, 16, or 32.<br>`Rmask`：a pointer filled in with the red mask for the format.<br>`Gmask`：a pointer filled in with the green mask for the format.<br>`Bmask`：a pointer filled in with the blue mask for the format.<br>`Amask`：a pointer filled in with the alpha mask for the format.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-2f2b9d541b42b5da"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetMaxHapticEffects(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40596) | Get the number of effects a haptic device can store. On some platforms this isn't fully supported, and therefore is an approximation. Always check to see if your created effect was actually created and do not rely solely on SDL_GetMaxHapticEffects(). @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetMaxHapticEffectsPlaying @sa SDL_GetHapticFeatures<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to query.<br>返回：the number of effects the haptic device can store or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7b7c1d730ee8cf26"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetMaxHapticEffectsPlaying(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52513) | Get the number of effects a haptic device can play at the same time. This is not supported on all platforms, but will always return a value. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetMaxHapticEffects @sa SDL_GetHapticFeatures<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to query maximum playing effects.<br>返回：the number of effects the haptic device can play at the same time or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-6bd9d4e3fc3ca116"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetMemoryFunctions(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<ulong, void*>* malloc_func, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<ulong, ulong, void*>* calloc_func, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, ulong, void*>* realloc_func, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, void>* free_func)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28450) | Get the current set of SDL memory functions. @threadsafety This does not hold a lock, so do not call this in the unlikely event of a background thread calling SDL_SetMemoryFunctions simultaneously. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetMemoryFunctions @sa SDL_GetOriginalMemoryFunctions<br>`malloc_func`：filled with malloc function.<br>`calloc_func`：filled with calloc function.<br>`realloc_func`：filled with realloc function.<br>`free_func`：filled with free function. |
| <a id="api-0985cef3be1f6938"></a>[`public uint* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetMice(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25326) | Get a list of currently connected mice. Note that this will include any device or virtual driver that includes mouse functionality, including some game controllers, KVM switches, etc. You should wait for input from a device before you consider it actively in use. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetMouseNameForID @sa SDL_HasMouse<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of mice returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a 0 terminated array of mouse instance IDs or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-dcfa1eb3e3d69d8c"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetModState()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31323) | Get the current key modifier state for the keyboard. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyboardState @sa SDL_SetModState<br>返回：an OR'd combination of the modifier keys for the keyboard. |
| <a id="api-e3a4953c7881f8a0"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetMouseFocus()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52612) | Get the window which currently has mouse focus. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the window with mouse focus. |
| <a id="api-5dc9bb3c9ab5e032"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetMouseNameForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25838) | Get the name of a mouse. This function returns "" if the mouse doesn't have a name. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetMice<br>`instance_id`：the mouse instance ID.<br>返回：the name of the selected mouse, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7fd2278ea9dcfcb1"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetMouseState(float* x, float* y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45710) | Query SDL's cache for the synchronous mouse button state and the window-relative SDL-cursor position. This function returns the cached synchronous state as SDL understands it from the last pump of the event queue. To query the platform for immediate asynchronous state, use SDL_GetGlobalMouseState. Passing non-NULL pointers to `x` or `y` will write the destination with respective x or y coordinates relative to the focused window. In Relative Mode, the SDL-cursor's position usually contradicts the platform-cursor's position as manually calculated from SDL_GetGlobalMouseState() and SDL_GetWindowPosition. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGlobalMouseState @sa SDL_GetRelativeMouseState<br>`x`：a pointer to receive the SDL-cursor's x-position from the focused window's top left corner, can be NULL if unused.<br>`y`：a pointer to receive the SDL-cursor's y-position from the focused window's top left corner, can be NULL if unused.<br>返回：a 32-bit bitmask of the button state that can be bitwise-compared against the SDL_BUTTON_MASK(X) macro. |
| <a id="api-a3a031aa2790e812"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayOrientation Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNaturalDisplayOrientation(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36304) | Get the orientation of a display when it is unrotated. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>返回：the SDL_DisplayOrientation enum value of the display, or `SDL_ORIENTATION_UNKNOWN` if it isn't available. |
| <a id="api-50c76b3bedb3c7ca"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumAllocations()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23044) | Get the number of outstanding (unfreed) allocations. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the number of allocations or -1 if allocation counting is disabled. |
| <a id="api-1053df25b6ea5c82"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumAudioDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41850) | Use this function to get the number of built-in audio drivers. This function returns a hardcoded number. This never returns a negative value; if there are no drivers compiled into this build of SDL, this function returns zero. The presence of a driver in this list does not mean it will function, it just means SDL is capable of interacting with that interface. For example, a build of SDL might have esound support, but if there's no esound server available, SDL's esound driver would fail if used. By default, SDL tries all drivers, in its preferred order, until one is found to be usable. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAudioDriver<br>返回：the number of built-in audio drivers. |
| <a id="api-3268c633a7f21e55"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumCameraDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39714) | Use this function to get the number of built-in camera drivers. This function returns a hardcoded number. This never returns a negative value; if there are no drivers compiled into this build of SDL, this function returns zero. The presence of a driver in this list does not mean it will function, it just means SDL is capable of interacting with that interface. For example, a build of SDL might have v4l2 support, but if there's no kernel support available, SDL's v4l2 driver would fail if used. By default, SDL tries all drivers, in its preferred order, until one is found to be usable. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCameraDriver<br>返回：the number of built-in camera drivers. |
| <a id="api-1e94a60899ddfb8b"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumGamepadTouchpadFingers(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, int touchpad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40621) | Get the number of supported simultaneous fingers on a touchpad on a game gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadTouchpadFinger @sa SDL_GetNumGamepadTouchpads<br>`gamepad`：a gamepad.<br>`touchpad`：a touchpad.<br>返回：number of supported simultaneous fingers. |
| <a id="api-2a16e6be1b7a9b9f"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumGamepadTouchpads(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51680) | Get the number of touchpads on a gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumGamepadTouchpadFingers<br>`gamepad`：a gamepad.<br>返回：number of touchpads. |
| <a id="api-4e1afb5a11755d81"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumGpuDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27531) | Get the number of GPU drivers compiled into SDL. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGPUDriver<br>返回：the number of built in GPU drivers. |
| <a id="api-4e94dbb68dd2afb8"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumHapticAxes(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48722) | Get the number of haptic axes the device has. The number of haptic axes might be useful if working with the SDL_HapticDirection effect. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to query.<br>返回：the number of axes on success or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7e41bdaeb1367e07"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumJoystickAxes(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39167) | Get the number of general axis controls on a joystick. Often, the directional pad on a game controller will either look like 4 separate buttons or a POV hat, and not axes, but all of this is up to the device and platform. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickAxis @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickBalls @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickButtons @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickHats<br>`joystick`：an SDL_Joystick structure containing joystick information.<br>返回：the number of axis controls/number of axes on success or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-dec67079587a40ed"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumJoystickBalls(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36912) | Get the number of trackballs on a joystick. Joystick trackballs have only relative motion events associated with them and their state cannot be polled. Most joysticks do not have trackballs. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickBall @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickAxes @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickButtons @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickHats<br>`joystick`：an SDL_Joystick structure containing joystick information.<br>返回：the number of trackballs on success or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-846b1cf58223dfae"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumJoystickButtons(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45958) | Get the number of buttons on a joystick. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickButton @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickAxes @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickBalls @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickHats<br>`joystick`：an SDL_Joystick structure containing joystick information.<br>返回：the number of buttons on success or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-52bb74da744dddc7"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumJoystickHats(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43986) | Get the number of POV hats on a joystick. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickHat @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickAxes @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickBalls @sa SDL_GetNumJoystickButtons<br>`joystick`：an SDL_Joystick structure containing joystick information.<br>返回：the number of POV hats on success or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-876f2b4f39503c85"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumLogicalCpuCores()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24458) | Get the number of logical CPU cores available. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the total number of logical CPU cores. On CPUs that include technologies such as hyperthreading, the number of logical cores may be more than the number of physical cores. |
| <a id="api-4c77fdba694f4026"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumRenderDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44769) | Get the number of 2D rendering drivers available for the current display. A render driver is a set of code that handles rendering and texture management on a particular display. Normally there is only one, but some drivers may have several available with different capabilities. There may be none if SDL was compiled without render support. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateRenderer @sa SDL_GetRenderDriver<br>返回：the number of built in render drivers. |
| <a id="api-4b87600b07487647"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumVideoDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48739) | Get the number of video drivers compiled into SDL. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetVideoDriver<br>返回：the number of built in video drivers. |
| <a id="api-ff16d48dc2f61df5"></a>[`public long Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetNumberProperty(uint props, sbyte* name, long default_value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37297) | Get a number property from a group of properties. You can use SDL_GetPropertyType() to query whether the property exists and is a number property. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPropertyType @sa SDL_HasProperty @sa SDL_SetNumberProperty<br>`props`：the properties to query.<br>`name`：the name of the property to query.<br>`default_value`：the default value of the property.<br>返回：the value of the property, or `default_value` if it is not set or not a number property. |
| <a id="api-63d78f9969a7ff33"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetOriginalMemoryFunctions(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<ulong, void*>* malloc_func, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<ulong, ulong, void*>* calloc_func, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, ulong, void*>* realloc_func, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, void>* free_func)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46244) | Get the original set of SDL memory functions. This is what SDL_malloc and friends will use by default, if there has been no call to SDL_SetMemoryFunctions. This is not necessarily using the C runtime's `malloc` functions behind the scenes! Different platforms and build configurations might do any number of unexpected things. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`malloc_func`：filled with malloc function.<br>`calloc_func`：filled with calloc function.<br>`realloc_func`：filled with realloc function.<br>`free_func`：filled with free function. |
| <a id="api-a97173c35104d693"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPathInfo(sbyte* path, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPathInfo* info)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30685) | Get information about a filesystem path. Symlinks, on filesystems that support them, are always followed, so you will always get information on what the symlink eventually points to, and not the symlink itself. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`path`：the path to query.<br>`info`：a pointer filled in with information about the path, or NULL to check for the existence of a file.<br>返回：true on success or false if the file doesn't exist, or another failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5d155c2eb42cf579"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPenDeviceType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPenDeviceType(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51280) | Get the device type of the given pen. Many platforms do not supply this information, so an app must always be prepared to get an SDL_PEN_DEVICE_TYPE_UNKNOWN result. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`instance_id`：the pen instance ID.<br>返回：the device type of the given pen, or SDL_PEN_DEVICE_TYPE_INVALID on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e862d9339d166366"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPerformanceCounter()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27358) | Get the current value of the high resolution counter. This function is typically used for profiling. The counter values are only meaningful relative to each other. Differences between values can be converted to times by using SDL_GetPerformanceFrequency(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPerformanceFrequency<br>返回：the current counter value. |
| <a id="api-6f15b11828dd925b"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPerformanceFrequency()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38704) | Get the count per second of the high resolution counter. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPerformanceCounter<br>返回：a platform-specific count per second. |
| <a id="api-6ea7c76a38606244"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormatDetails* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPixelFormatDetails(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20770) | Create an SDL_PixelFormatDetails structure corresponding to a pixel format. Returned structure may come from a shared global cache (i.e. not newly allocated), and hence should not be modified, especially the palette. Weird errors such as `Blit combination not supported` may occur. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`format`：one of the SDL_PixelFormat values.<br>返回：a pointer to a SDL_PixelFormatDetails structure or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-10ba155e5ee0a4d5"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPixelFormatForMasks(int bpp, uint Rmask, uint Gmask, uint Bmask, uint Amask)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31585) | Convert a bpp value and RGBA masks to an enumerated pixel format. This will return `SDL_PIXELFORMAT_UNKNOWN` if the conversion wasn't possible. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetMasksForPixelFormat<br>`bpp`：a bits per pixel value; usually 15, 16, or 32.<br>`Rmask`：the red mask for the format.<br>`Gmask`：the green mask for the format.<br>`Bmask`：the blue mask for the format.<br>`Amask`：the alpha mask for the format.<br>返回：the SDL_PixelFormat value corresponding to the format masks, or SDL_PIXELFORMAT_UNKNOWN if there isn't a match. |
| <a id="api-e4d18abe9e56ccec"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPixelFormatFromGpuTextureFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27679) | Get the SDL pixel format corresponding to a GPU texture format. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`format`：a texture format.<br>返回：the corresponding pixel format, or SDL_PIXELFORMAT_UNKNOWN if there is no corresponding pixel format. |
| <a id="api-03b62932e2afeacb"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPixelFormatName(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46499) | Get the human readable name of a pixel format. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`format`：the pixel format to query.<br>返回：the human readable name of the specified pixel format or "SDL_PIXELFORMAT_UNKNOWN" if the format isn't recognized. |
| <a id="api-ed58ae781f8a9189"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPlatform()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40013) | Get the name of the platform. Here are the names returned for some (but not all) supported platforms: - "Windows" - "macOS" - "Linux" - "iOS" - "Android" @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the name of the platform. If the correct platform name is not available, returns a string beginning with the text "Unknown". |
| <a id="api-8e91863cdbff9490"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPointerProperty(uint props, sbyte* name, void* default_value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23508) | Get a pointer property from a group of properties. By convention, the names of properties that SDL exposes on objects will start with "SDL.", and properties that SDL uses internally will start with "SDL.internal.".
