These should be considered read-only and should not be modified by applications. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, although the data returned is not protected and could potentially be freed if you call SDL_SetPointerProperty() or SDL_ClearProperty() on these properties from another thread. If you need to avoid this, use SDL_LockProperties() and SDL_UnlockProperties(). @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetBooleanProperty @sa SDL_GetFloatProperty @sa SDL_GetNumberProperty @sa SDL_GetPropertyType @sa SDL_GetStringProperty @sa SDL_HasProperty @sa SDL_SetPointerProperty<br>`props`：the properties to query.<br>`name`：the name of the property to query.<br>`default_value`：the default value of the property.<br>返回：the value of the property, or `default_value` if it is not set or not a pointer property. |
| <a id="api-bd13d74927a696d2"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPowerState Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPowerInfo(int* seconds, int* percent)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41558) | Get the current power supply details. You should never take a battery status as absolute truth. Batteries (especially failing batteries) are delicate hardware, and the values reported here are best estimates based on what that hardware reports. It's not uncommon for older batteries to lose stored power much faster than it reports, or completely drain when reporting it has 20 percent left, etc. Battery status can change at any time; if you are concerned with power state, you should call this function frequently, and perhaps ignore changes until they seem to be stable for a few seconds. It's possible a platform can only report battery percentage or time left but not both. On some platforms, retrieving power supply details might be expensive. If you want to display continuous status you could call this function every minute or so. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`seconds`：a pointer filled in with the seconds of battery life left, or NULL to ignore. This will be filled in with -1 if we can't determine a value or there is no battery.<br>`percent`：a pointer filled in with the percentage of battery life left, between 0 and 100, or NULL to ignore. This will be filled in with -1 when we can't determine a value or there is no battery.<br>返回：the current battery state or `SDL_POWERSTATE_ERROR` on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-290d7b23585e14e5"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPrefPath(sbyte* org, sbyte* app)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32865) | Get the user-and-app-specific path where files can be written. Get the "pref dir". This is meant to be where users can write personal files (preferences and save games, etc) that are specific to your application. This directory is unique per user, per application. This function will decide the appropriate location in the native filesystem, create the directory if necessary, and return a string of the absolute path to the directory in UTF-8 encoding. On Windows, the string might look like: `C: \ Users \ bob \ AppData \ Roaming \ My Company \ My Program Name \ ` On Linux, the string might look like: `/home/bob/.local/share/My Program Name/` On macOS, the string might look like: `/Users/bob/Library/Application Support/My Program Name/` You should assume the path returned by this function is the only safe place to write files (and that SDL_GetBasePath(), while it might be writable, or even the parent of the returned path, isn't where you should be writing things). Both the org and app strings may become part of a directory name, so please follow these rules: - Try to use the same org string (_including case-sensitivity_) for all your applications that use this function. - Always use a unique app string for each one, and make sure it never changes for an app once you've decided on it. - Unicode characters are legal, as long as they are UTF-8 encoded, but... - ...only use letters, numbers, and spaces. Avoid punctuation like "Game Name 2: Bad Guy's Revenge!" ... "Game Name 2" is sufficient. Due to historical mistakes, `org` is allowed to be NULL or "". In such cases, SDL will omit the org subdirectory, including on platforms where it shouldn't, and including on platforms where this would make your app fail certification for an app store. New apps should definitely specify a real string for `org`. The returned path is guaranteed to end with a path separator (' \ ' on Windows, '/' on most other platforms). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetBasePath<br>`org`：the name of your organization.<br>`app`：the name of your application.<br>返回：a UTF-8 string of the user directory in platform-dependent notation. NULL if there's a problem (creating directory failed, etc.). This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-e15f38cb5f9708f8"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLLocale** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPreferredLocales(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46591) | Report the user's preferred locale. Returned language strings are in the format xx, where 'xx' is an ISO-639 language specifier (such as "en" for English, "de" for German, etc). Country strings are in the format YY, where "YY" is an ISO-3166 country code (such as "US" for the United States, "CA" for Canada, etc). Country might be NULL if there's no specific guidance on them (so you might get { "en", "US" } for American English, but { "en", NULL } means "English language, generically"). Language strings are never NULL, except to terminate the array. Please note that not all of these strings are 2 characters; some are three or more. The returned list of locales are in the order of the user's preference. For example, a German citizen that is fluent in US English and knows enough Japanese to navigate around Tokyo might have a list like: { "de", "en_US", "jp", NULL }. Someone from England might prefer British English (where "color" is spelled "colour", etc), but will settle for anything like it: { "en_GB", "en", NULL }. This function returns NULL on error, including when the platform does not supply this information at all. This might be a "slow" call that has to query the operating system. It's best to ask for this once and save the results. However, this list can change, usually because the user has changed a system preference outside of your program; SDL will send an SDL_EVENT_LOCALE_CHANGED event in this case, if possible, and you can call this function again to get an updated copy of preferred locales. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of locales returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a NULL terminated array of locale pointers, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This is a single allocation that should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-99989ddf2b0b6c60"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPrimaryDisplay()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37423) | Return the primary display. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>返回：the instance ID of the primary display on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-373a7afde5e9f0f3"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPrimarySelectionText()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33074) | Get UTF-8 text from the primary selection. This function returns an empty string if there is not enough memory left for a copy of the primary selection's content. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasPrimarySelectionText @sa SDL_SetPrimarySelectionText<br>返回：the primary selection text on success or an empty string on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-380e8129934bab4b"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetProcessInput(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLProcess* process)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32339) | Get the SDL_IOStream associated with process standard input. The process must have been created with SDL_CreateProcess() and pipe_stdio set to true, or with SDL_CreateProcessWithProperties() and `SDL_PROP_PROCESS_CREATE_STDIN_NUMBER` set to `SDL_PROCESS_STDIO_APP`. Writing to this stream can return less data than expected if the process hasn't read its input. It may be blocked waiting for its output to be read, if so you may need to call SDL_GetProcessOutput() and read the output in parallel with writing input. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateProcess @sa SDL_CreateProcessWithProperties @sa SDL_GetProcessOutput<br>`process`：The process to get the input stream for.<br>返回：the input stream or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4791e2dd387a4510"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetProcessOutput(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLProcess* process)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23938) | Get the SDL_IOStream associated with process standard output. The process must have been created with SDL_CreateProcess() and pipe_stdio set to true, or with SDL_CreateProcessWithProperties() and `SDL_PROP_PROCESS_CREATE_STDOUT_NUMBER` set to `SDL_PROCESS_STDIO_APP`. Reading from this stream can return 0 with SDL_GetIOStatus() returning SDL_IO_STATUS_NOT_READY if no output is available yet. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateProcess @sa SDL_CreateProcessWithProperties @sa SDL_GetProcessInput<br>`process`：The process to get the output stream for.<br>返回：the output stream or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-be8b6399d4e3650a"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetProcessProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLProcess* process)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47011) | Get the properties associated with a process. The following read-only properties are provided by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_PROCESS_PID_NUMBER`: the process ID of the process. - `SDL_PROP_PROCESS_STDIN_POINTER`: an SDL_IOStream that can be used to write input to the process, if it was created with `SDL_PROP_PROCESS_CREATE_STDIN_NUMBER` set to `SDL_PROCESS_STDIO_APP`. - `SDL_PROP_PROCESS_STDOUT_POINTER`: a non-blocking SDL_IOStream that can be used to read output from the process, if it was created with `SDL_PROP_PROCESS_CREATE_STDOUT_NUMBER` set to `SDL_PROCESS_STDIO_APP`. - `SDL_PROP_PROCESS_STDERR_POINTER`: a non-blocking SDL_IOStream that can be used to read error output from the process, if it was created with `SDL_PROP_PROCESS_CREATE_STDERR_NUMBER` set to `SDL_PROCESS_STDIO_APP`. - `SDL_PROP_PROCESS_BACKGROUND_BOOLEAN`: true if the process is running in the background. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateProcess @sa SDL_CreateProcessWithProperties<br>`process`：the process to query.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-43bb4e7195b2b3b3"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPropertyType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetPropertyType(uint props, sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43001) | Get the type of a property in a group of properties. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasProperty<br>`props`：the properties to query.<br>`name`：the name of the property to query.<br>返回：the type of the property, or SDL_PROPERTY_TYPE_INVALID if it is not set. |
| <a id="api-eff6406513c8dc5c"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRealGamepadType(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38649) | Get the type of an opened gamepad, ignoring any mapping override. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRealGamepadTypeForID<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad object to query.<br>返回：the gamepad type, or SDL_GAMEPAD_TYPE_UNKNOWN if it's not available. |
| <a id="api-da1d215283eec641"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRealGamepadTypeForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34217) | Get the type of a gamepad, ignoring any mapping override. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadTypeForID @sa SDL_GetGamepads @sa SDL_GetRealGamepadType<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the gamepad type. |
| <a id="api-41a2fc51ea747e42"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRectAndLineIntersection(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect, int* X1, int* Y1, int* X2, int* Y2)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33598) | Calculate the intersection of a rectangle and line segment. This function is used to clip a line segment to a rectangle. A line segment contained entirely within the rectangle or that does not intersect will remain unchanged. A line segment that crosses the rectangle at either or both ends will be clipped to the boundary of the rectangle and the new coordinates saved in `X1`, `Y1`, `X2`, and/or `Y2` as necessary. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`rect`：an SDL_Rect structure representing the rectangle to intersect.<br>`X1`：a pointer to the starting X-coordinate of the line.<br>`Y1`：a pointer to the starting Y-coordinate of the line.<br>`X2`：a pointer to the ending X-coordinate of the line.<br>`Y2`：a pointer to the ending Y-coordinate of the line.<br>返回：true if there is an intersection, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-77baf2416504fdf1"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRectAndLineIntersectionFloat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* rect, float* X1, float* Y1, float* X2, float* Y2)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21018) | Calculate the intersection of a rectangle and line segment with float precision. This function is used to clip a line segment to a rectangle. A line segment contained entirely within the rectangle or that does not intersect will remain unchanged. A line segment that crosses the rectangle at either or both ends will be clipped to the boundary of the rectangle and the new coordinates saved in `X1`, `Y1`, `X2`, and/or `Y2` as necessary. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`rect`：an SDL_FRect structure representing the rectangle to intersect.<br>`X1`：a pointer to the starting X-coordinate of the line.<br>`Y1`：a pointer to the starting Y-coordinate of the line.<br>`X2`：a pointer to the ending X-coordinate of the line.<br>`Y2`：a pointer to the ending Y-coordinate of the line.<br>返回：true if there is an intersection, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-a336cc1b78ac3457"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRectEnclosingPoints(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPoint* points, int count, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* clip, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* result)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50831) | Calculate a minimal rectangle enclosing a set of points. If `clip` is not NULL then only points inside of the clipping rectangle are considered. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`points`：an array of SDL_Point structures representing points to be enclosed.<br>`count`：the number of structures in the `points` array.<br>`clip`：an SDL_Rect used for clipping or NULL to enclose all points.<br>`result`：an SDL_Rect structure filled in with the minimal enclosing rectangle.<br>返回：true if any points were enclosed or false if all the points were outside of the clipping rectangle. |
| <a id="api-33829a1b908ec220"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRectEnclosingPointsFloat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFPoint* points, int count, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* clip, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* result)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46278) | Calculate a minimal rectangle enclosing a set of points with float precision. If `clip` is not NULL then only points inside of the clipping rectangle are considered. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`points`：an array of SDL_FPoint structures representing points to be enclosed.<br>`count`：the number of structures in the `points` array.<br>`clip`：an SDL_FRect used for clipping or NULL to enclose all points.<br>`result`：an SDL_FRect structure filled in with the minimal enclosing rectangle.<br>返回：true if any points were enclosed or false if all the points were outside of the clipping rectangle. |
| <a id="api-3626b80c68c15573"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRectIntersection(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* A, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* B, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* result)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20570) | Calculate the intersection of two rectangles. If `result` is NULL then this function will return false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasRectIntersection<br>`A`：an SDL_Rect structure representing the first rectangle.<br>`B`：an SDL_Rect structure representing the second rectangle.<br>`result`：an SDL_Rect structure filled in with the intersection of rectangles `A` and `B`.<br>返回：true if there is an intersection, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-2adf4c55284a7770"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRectIntersectionFloat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* A, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* B, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* result)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42140) | Calculate the intersection of two rectangles with float precision. If `result` is NULL then this function will return false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasRectIntersectionFloat<br>`A`：an SDL_FRect structure representing the first rectangle.<br>`B`：an SDL_FRect structure representing the second rectangle.<br>`result`：an SDL_FRect structure filled in with the intersection of rectangles `A` and `B`.<br>返回：true if there is an intersection, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-18d8dc6b033498b7"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRectUnion(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* A, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* B, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* result)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41959) | Calculate the union of two rectangles. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`A`：an SDL_Rect structure representing the first rectangle.<br>`B`：an SDL_Rect structure representing the second rectangle.<br>`result`：an SDL_Rect structure filled in with the union of rectangles `A` and `B`.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d4ab5343bea5b9fa"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRectUnionFloat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* A, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* B, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* result)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28046) | Calculate the union of two rectangles with float precision. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`A`：an SDL_FRect structure representing the first rectangle.<br>`B`：an SDL_FRect structure representing the second rectangle.<br>`result`：an SDL_FRect structure filled in with the union of rectangles `A` and `B`.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ee4793b19aff2d66"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRelativeMouseState(float* x, float* y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52853) | Query SDL's cache for the synchronous mouse button state and accumulated mouse delta since last call. This function returns the cached synchronous state as SDL understands it from the last pump of the event queue. To query the platform for immediate asynchronous state, use SDL_GetGlobalMouseState. Passing non-NULL pointers to `x` or `y` will write the destination with respective x or y deltas accumulated since the last call to this function (or since event initialization). This function is useful for reducing overhead by processing relative mouse inputs in one go per-frame instead of individually per-event, at the expense of losing the order between events within the frame (e.g. quickly pressing and releasing a button within the same frame). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetMouseState @sa SDL_GetGlobalMouseState<br>`x`：a pointer to receive the x mouse delta accumulated since last call, can be NULL if unused.<br>`y`：a pointer to receive the y mouse delta accumulated since last call, can be NULL if unused.<br>返回：a 32-bit bitmask of the button state that can be bitwise-compared against the SDL_BUTTON_MASK(X) macro. |
| <a id="api-d636e01a64f1fd44"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderClipRect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44014) | Get the clip rectangle for the current target. Each render target has its own clip rectangle. This function gets the cliprect for the current render target. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderClipEnabled @sa SDL_SetRenderClipRect<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`rect`：an SDL_Rect structure filled in with the current clipping area or an empty rectangle if clipping is disabled.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-988043f788821e3a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderColorScale(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, float* scale)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38441) | Get the color scale used for render operations. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderColorScale<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`scale`：a pointer filled in with the current color scale value.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c755a31ed874cb19"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderDrawBlendMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, uint* blendMode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17027) | Get the blend mode used for drawing operations. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderDrawBlendMode<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`blendMode`：a pointer filled in with the current SDL_BlendMode.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4d572d7105d9159d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderDrawColor(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, byte* r, byte* g, byte* b, byte* a)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46723) | Get the color used for drawing operations (Rect, Line and Clear). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderDrawColorFloat @sa SDL_SetRenderDrawColor<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`r`：a pointer filled in with the red value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`g`：a pointer filled in with the green value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`b`：a pointer filled in with the blue value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`a`：a pointer filled in with the alpha value used to draw on the rendering target; usually `SDL_ALPHA_OPAQUE` (255).<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-748ed57177445c61"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderDrawColorFloat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, float* r, float* g, float* b, float* a)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34044) | Get the color used for drawing operations (Rect, Line and Clear). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderDrawColorFloat @sa SDL_GetRenderDrawColor<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`r`：a pointer filled in with the red value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`g`：a pointer filled in with the green value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`b`：a pointer filled in with the blue value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`a`：a pointer filled in with the alpha value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d1909bd7c96b1783"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderDriver(int index)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36142) | Use this function to get the name of a built in 2D rendering driver. The list of rendering drivers is given in the order that they are normally initialized by default; the drivers that seem more reasonable to choose first (as far as the SDL developers believe) are earlier in the list. The names of drivers are all simple, low-ASCII identifiers, like "opengl", "direct3d12" or "metal". These never have Unicode characters, and are not meant to be proper names. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumRenderDrivers<br>`index`：the index of the rendering driver; the value ranges from 0 to SDL_GetNumRenderDrivers() - 1.<br>返回：the name of the rendering driver at the requested index, or NULL if an invalid index was specified. |
| <a id="api-db07bb6573572892"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderLogicalPresentation(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, int* w, int* h, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRendererLogicalPresentation* mode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42547) | Get device independent resolution and presentation mode for rendering. This function gets the width and height of the logical rendering output, or 0 if a logical resolution is not enabled. Each render target has its own logical presentation state. This function gets the state for the current render target. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderLogicalPresentation<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`w`：an int filled with the logical presentation width.<br>`h`：an int filled with the logical presentation height.<br>`mode`：a variable filled with the logical presentation mode being used.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d13598b7b011cc43"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderLogicalPresentationRect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31459) | Get the final presentation rectangle for rendering. This function returns the calculated rectangle used for logical presentation, based on the presentation mode and output size. If logical presentation is disabled, it will fill the rectangle with the output size, in pixels. Each render target has its own logical presentation state. This function gets the rectangle for the current render target. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderLogicalPresentation<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`rect`：a pointer filled in with the final presentation rectangle, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b253838c0d17c093"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderMetalCommandEncoder(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29921) | Get the Metal command encoder for the current frame. This function returns `void *`, so SDL doesn't have to include Metal's headers, but it can be safely cast to an `id <MTLRenderCommandEncoder >`. This will return NULL if Metal refuses to give SDL a drawable to render to, which might happen if the window is hidden/minimized/offscreen. This doesn't apply to command encoders for render targets, just the window's backbuffer. Check your return values! @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderMetalLayer<br>`renderer`：the renderer to query.<br>返回：an `id <MTLRenderCommandEncoder >` on success, or NULL if the renderer isn't a Metal renderer or there was an error. |
| <a id="api-7af9f012e54c8013"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderMetalLayer(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22773) | Get the CAMetalLayer associated with the given Metal renderer. This function returns `void *`, so SDL doesn't have to include Metal's headers, but it can be safely cast to a `CAMetalLayer *`. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderMetalCommandEncoder<br>`renderer`：the renderer to query.<br>返回：a `CAMetalLayer *` on success, or NULL if the renderer isn't a Metal renderer. |
| <a id="api-a5a3a03b177fbf43"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderOutputSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, int* w, int* h)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31817) | Get the output size in pixels of a rendering context. This returns the true output size in pixels, ignoring any render targets or logical size and presentation. For the output size of the current rendering target, with logical size adjustments, use SDL_GetCurrentRenderOutputSize() instead. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCurrentRenderOutputSize<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`w`：a pointer filled in with the width in pixels.<br>`h`：a pointer filled in with the height in pixels.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e284f3aee6fafe9a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderSafeArea(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31489) | Get the safe area for rendering within the current viewport. Some devices have portions of the screen which are partially obscured or not interactive, possibly due to on-screen controls, curved edges, camera notches, TV overscan, etc. This function provides the area of the current viewport which is safe to have interactible content. You should continue rendering into the rest of the render target, but it should not contain visually important or interactible content. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`rect`：a pointer filled in with the area that is safe for interactive content.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-86d13cea95e90870"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderScale(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, float* scaleX, float* scaleY)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26657) | Get the drawing scale for the current target. Each render target has its own scale. This function gets the scale for the current render target. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderScale<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`scaleX`：a pointer filled in with the horizontal scaling factor.<br>`scaleY`：a pointer filled in with the vertical scaling factor.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a9758d5503198e54"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderTarget(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18525) | Get the current render target. The default render target is the window for which the renderer was created, and is reported a NULL here. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderTarget<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>返回：the current render target or NULL for the default render target. |
| <a id="api-43b0d9e944dc5431"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderTextureAddressMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTextureAddressMode* u_mode, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTextureAddressMode* v_mode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44098) | Get the texture addressing mode used in SDL_RenderGeometry(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderTextureAddressMode<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`u_mode`：a pointer filled in with the SDL_TextureAddressMode to use for horizontal texture coordinates in SDL_RenderGeometry(), may be NULL.<br>`v_mode`：a pointer filled in with the SDL_TextureAddressMode to use for vertical texture coordinates in SDL_RenderGeometry(), may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c6cf671d1f310e0c"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderVSync(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, int* vsync)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43887) | Get VSync of the given renderer. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderVSync<br>`renderer`：the renderer to toggle.<br>`vsync`：an int filled with the current vertical refresh sync interval. See SDL_SetRenderVSync() for the meaning of the value.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-87d95808e6b7fea3"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderViewport(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34756) | Get the drawing area for the current target. Each render target has its own viewport. This function gets the viewport for the current render target. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderViewportSet @sa SDL_SetRenderViewport<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`rect`：an SDL_Rect structure filled in with the current drawing area.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-14de741ecb6d62f7"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38628) | Get the window associated with a renderer. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`renderer`：the renderer to query.<br>返回：the window on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-520de71698f6bbe1"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRenderer(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41796) | Get the renderer associated with a window. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：the rendering context on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-84d540a27e60005f"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRendererFromTexture(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42206) | Get the renderer that created an SDL_Texture. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`texture`：the texture to query.<br>返回：a pointer to the SDL_Renderer that created the texture, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-64cf1ff68aec17a7"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRendererName(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43703) | Get the name of a renderer. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateRenderer @sa SDL_CreateRendererWithProperties<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>返回：the name of the selected renderer, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-804567c27536d93b"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRendererProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21203) | Get the properties associated with a renderer. The following read-only properties are provided by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_NAME_STRING`: the name of the rendering driver - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_WINDOW_POINTER`: the window where rendering is displayed, if any - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_SURFACE_POINTER`: the surface where rendering is displayed, if this is a software renderer without a window - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_VSYNC_NUMBER`: the current vsync setting - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_MAX_TEXTURE_SIZE_NUMBER`: the maximum texture width and height - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_TEXTURE_FORMATS_POINTER`: a (const SDL_PixelFormat *) array of pixel formats, terminated with SDL_PIXELFORMAT_UNKNOWN, representing the available texture formats for this renderer. - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_TEXTURE_WRAPPING_BOOLEAN`: true if the renderer supports SDL_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_WRAP on non-power-of-two textures. - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_OUTPUT_COLORSPACE_NUMBER`: an SDL_Colorspace value describing the colorspace for output to the display, defaults to SDL_COLORSPACE_SRGB. - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_HDR_ENABLED_BOOLEAN`: true if the output colorspace is SDL_COLORSPACE_SRGB_LINEAR and the renderer is showing on a display with HDR enabled. This property can change dynamically when SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_HDR_STATE_CHANGED is sent. - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_SDR_WHITE_POINT_FLOAT`: the value of SDR white in the SDL_COLORSPACE_SRGB_LINEAR colorspace. When HDR is enabled, this value is automatically multiplied into the color scale. This property can change dynamically when SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_HDR_STATE_CHANGED is sent. - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_HDR_HEADROOM_FLOAT`: the additional high dynamic range that can be displayed, in terms of the SDR white point. When HDR is not enabled, this will be 1.0. This property can change dynamically when SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_HDR_STATE_CHANGED is sent. With the direct3d renderer: - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_D3D9_DEVICE_POINTER`: the IDirect3DDevice9 associated with the renderer With the direct3d11 renderer: - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_D3D11_DEVICE_POINTER`: the ID3D11Device associated with the renderer - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_D3D11_SWAPCHAIN_POINTER`: the IDXGISwapChain1 associated with the renderer. This may change when the window is resized. With the direct3d12 renderer: - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_D3D12_DEVICE_POINTER`: the ID3D12Device associated with the renderer - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_D3D12_SWAPCHAIN_POINTER`: the IDXGISwapChain4 associated with the renderer. - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_D3D12_COMMAND_QUEUE_POINTER`: the ID3D12CommandQueue associated with the renderer With the vulkan renderer: - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_VULKAN_INSTANCE_POINTER`: the VkInstance associated with the renderer - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_VULKAN_SURFACE_NUMBER`: the VkSurfaceKHR associated with the renderer - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_VULKAN_PHYSICAL_DEVICE_POINTER`: the VkPhysicalDevice associated with the renderer - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_VULKAN_DEVICE_POINTER`: the VkDevice associated with the renderer - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_VULKAN_GRAPHICS_QUEUE_FAMILY_INDEX_NUMBER`: the queue family index used for rendering - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_VULKAN_PRESENT_QUEUE_FAMILY_INDEX_NUMBER`: the queue family index used for presentation - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_VULKAN_SWAPCHAIN_IMAGE_COUNT_NUMBER`: the number of swapchain images, or potential frames in flight, used by the Vulkan renderer With the gpu renderer: - `SDL_PROP_RENDERER_GPU_DEVICE_POINTER`: the SDL_GPUDevice associated with the renderer @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-1d22b77d55d5abf1"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRevision()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49800) | Get the code revision of the SDL library that is linked against your program. This value is the revision of the code you are linking against and may be different from the code you are compiling with, which is found in the constant SDL_REVISION if you explicitly include SDL_revision.h The revision is an arbitrary string (a hash value) uniquely identifying the exact revision of the SDL library in use, and is only useful in comparing against other revisions. It is NOT an incrementing number. If SDL wasn't built from a git repository with the appropriate tools, this will return an empty string. You shouldn't use this function for anything but logging it for debugging purposes. The string is not intended to be reliable in any way. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetVersion<br>返回：an arbitrary string, uniquely identifying the exact revision of the SDL library in use. |
| <a id="api-dfc8aaf38a70d506"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRgb(uint pixelvalue, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormatDetails* format, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPalette* palette, byte* r, byte* g, byte* b)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38560) | Get RGB values from a pixel in the specified format. This function uses the entire 8-bit [0..255] range when converting color components from pixel formats with less than 8-bits per RGB component (e.g., a completely white pixel in 16-bit RGB565 format would return [0xff, 0xff, 0xff] not [0xf8, 0xfc, 0xf8]). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as long as the palette is not modified. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPixelFormatDetails @sa SDL_GetRGBA @sa SDL_MapRGB @sa SDL_MapRGBA<br>`pixelvalue`：a pixel value.<br>`format`：a pointer to SDL_PixelFormatDetails describing the pixel format.<br>`palette`：an optional palette for indexed formats, may be NULL.<br>`r`：a pointer filled in with the red component, may be NULL.<br>`g`：a pointer filled in with the green component, may be NULL.<br>`b`：a pointer filled in with the blue component, may be NULL. |
| <a id="api-7cb2c19db09554d4"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetRgba(uint pixelvalue, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormatDetails* format, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPalette* palette, byte* r, byte* g, byte* b, byte* a)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47671) | Get RGBA values from a pixel in the specified format. This function uses the entire 8-bit [0..255] range when converting color components from pixel formats with less than 8-bits per RGB component (e.g., a completely white pixel in 16-bit RGB565 format would return [0xff, 0xff, 0xff] not [0xf8, 0xfc, 0xf8]). If the surface has no alpha component, the alpha will be returned as 0xff (100% opaque). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as long as the palette is not modified. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPixelFormatDetails @sa SDL_GetRGB @sa SDL_MapRGB @sa SDL_MapRGBA<br>`pixelvalue`：a pixel value.<br>`format`：a pointer to SDL_PixelFormatDetails describing the pixel format.<br>`palette`：an optional palette for indexed formats, may be NULL.<br>`r`：a pointer filled in with the red component, may be NULL.<br>`g`：a pointer filled in with the green component, may be NULL.<br>`b`：a pointer filled in with the blue component, may be NULL.<br>`a`：a pointer filled in with the alpha component, may be NULL. |
| <a id="api-d8d1f9722c7dd324"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSandbox Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSandbox()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24828) | Get the application sandbox environment, if any. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the application sandbox environment or SDL_SANDBOX_NONE if the application is not running in a sandbox environment. |
| <a id="api-07047a2ffd2d6461"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScancode Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetScancodeFromKey(uint key, ushort* modstate)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51582) | Get the scancode corresponding to the given key code according to the current keyboard layout. Note that there may be multiple scancode+modifier states that can generate this keycode, this will just return the first one found. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyFromScancode @sa SDL_GetScancodeName<br>`key`：the desired SDL_Keycode to query.<br>`modstate`：a pointer to the modifier state that would be used when the scancode generates this key, may be NULL.<br>返回：the SDL_Scancode that corresponds to the given SDL_Keycode. |
| <a id="api-498079fbfc6a37eb"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScancode Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetScancodeFromName(sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29482) | Get a scancode from a human-readable name. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyFromName @sa SDL_GetScancodeFromKey @sa SDL_GetScancodeName<br>`name`：the human-readable scancode name.<br>返回：the SDL_Scancode, or `SDL_SCANCODE_UNKNOWN` if the name wasn't recognized; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-799ab9dcc5e05499"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetScancodeName(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScancode scancode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45475) | Get a human-readable name for a scancode. **Warning**: The returned name is by design not stable across platforms, e.g. the name for `SDL_SCANCODE_LGUI` is "Left GUI" under Linux but "Left Windows" under Microsoft Windows, and some scancodes like `SDL_SCANCODE_NONUSBACKSLASH` don't have any name at all. There are even scancodes that share names, e.g. `SDL_SCANCODE_RETURN` and `SDL_SCANCODE_RETURN2` (both called "Return"). This function is therefore unsuitable for creating a stable cross-platform two-way mapping between strings and scancodes. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetScancodeFromKey @sa SDL_GetScancodeFromName @sa SDL_SetScancodeName<br>`scancode`：the desired SDL_Scancode to query.<br>返回：a pointer to the name for the scancode. If the scancode doesn't have a name this function returns an empty string (""). |
| <a id="api-5c4ce3fc760f0cab"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSemaphoreValue(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSemaphore* sem)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33538) | Get the current value of a semaphore. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`sem`：the semaphore to query.<br>返回：the current value of the semaphore. |
| <a id="api-52f93e0b5f211ebd"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSensorData(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensor* sensor, float* data, int num_values)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29843) | Get the current state of an opened sensor. The number of values and interpretation of the data is sensor dependent. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`sensor`：the SDL_Sensor object to query.<br>`data`：a pointer filled with the current sensor state.<br>`num_values`：the number of values to write to data.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-df46c8d6f061d518"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensor* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSensorFromId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39081) | Return the SDL_Sensor associated with an instance ID. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the sensor instance ID.<br>返回：an SDL_Sensor object or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a794235f3db18d22"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSensorId(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensor* sensor)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52532) | Get the instance ID of a sensor. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`sensor`：the SDL_Sensor object to inspect.<br>返回：the sensor instance ID, or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b9b8bebc9846b874"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSensorName(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensor* sensor)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36488) | Get the implementation dependent name of a sensor. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`sensor`：the SDL_Sensor object.<br>返回：the sensor name or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4e79167ea77c5341"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSensorNameForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41378) | Get the implementation dependent name of a sensor. This can be called before any sensors are opened. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the sensor instance ID.<br>返回：the sensor name, or NULL if `instance_id` is not valid. |
| <a id="api-0e089598ad267dee"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSensorNonPortableType(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensor* sensor)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29073) | Get the platform dependent type of a sensor. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`sensor`：the SDL_Sensor object to inspect.<br>返回：the sensor platform dependent type, or -1 if `sensor` is NULL. |
| <a id="api-e56e095e19268e65"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSensorNonPortableTypeForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25388) | Get the platform dependent type of a sensor. This can be called before any sensors are opened. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the sensor instance ID.<br>返回：the sensor platform dependent type, or -1 if `instance_id` is not valid. |
| <a id="api-73ee0ff9cd48c805"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSensorProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensor* sensor)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49352) | Get the properties associated with a sensor. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`sensor`：the SDL_Sensor object.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8a6adeafe54bfe3e"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensorType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSensorType(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensor* sensor)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21037) | Get the type of a sensor. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`sensor`：the SDL_Sensor object to inspect.<br>返回：the SDL_SensorType type, or `SDL_SENSOR_INVALID` if `sensor` is NULL. |
| <a id="api-28131934d76ecc10"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensorType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSensorTypeForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23528) | Get the type of a sensor. This can be called before any sensors are opened. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the sensor instance ID.<br>返回：the SDL_SensorType, or `SDL_SENSOR_INVALID` if `instance_id` is not valid. |
| <a id="api-0ffbaaa49b35f0dc"></a>[`public uint* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSensors(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23125) | Get a list of currently connected sensors. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of sensors returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a 0 terminated array of sensor instance IDs or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-8fad1b6475e1fd9a"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSilenceValueForFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39509) | Get the appropriate memset value for silencing an audio format. The value returned by this function can be used as the second argument to memset (or SDL_memset) to set an audio buffer in a specific format to silence. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`format`：the audio data format to query.<br>返回：a byte value that can be passed to memset. |
| <a id="api-61332a4438b46994"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSimdAlignment()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50380) | Report the alignment this system needs for SIMD allocations. This will return the minimum number of bytes to which a pointer must be aligned to be compatible with SIMD instructions on the current machine. For example, if the machine supports SSE only, it will return 16, but if it supports AVX-512F, it'll return 64 (etc). This only reports values for instruction sets SDL knows about, so if your SDL build doesn't have SDL_HasAVX512F(), then it might return 16 for the SSE support it sees and not 64 for the AVX-512 instructions that exist but SDL doesn't know about. Plan accordingly. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_aligned_alloc @sa SDL_aligned_free<br>返回：the alignment in bytes needed for available, known SIMD instructions. |
| <a id="api-bb1ef6d579cfa6c9"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetStorageFileSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLStorage* storage, sbyte* path, ulong* length)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40501) | Query the size of a file within a storage container. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ReadStorageFile @sa SDL_StorageReady<br>`storage`：a storage container to query.<br>`path`：the relative path of the file to query.<br>`length`：a pointer to be filled with the file's length.<br>返回：true if the file could be queried or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-2e00294c3ebab25b"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetStoragePathInfo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLStorage* storage, sbyte* path, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPathInfo* info)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22501) | Get information about a filesystem path in a storage container. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_StorageReady<br>`storage`：a storage container.<br>`path`：the path to query.<br>`info`：a pointer filled in with information about the path, or NULL to check for the existence of a file.<br>返回：true on success or false if the file doesn't exist, or another failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c6686b6735f85131"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetStorageSpaceRemaining(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLStorage* storage)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41753) | Queries the remaining space in a storage container. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_StorageReady @sa SDL_WriteStorageFile<br>`storage`：a storage container to query.<br>返回：the amount of remaining space, in bytes. |
| <a id="api-bb43487c3eb78582"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetStringProperty(uint props, sbyte* name, sbyte* default_value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33159) | Get a string property from a group of properties. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, although the data returned is not protected and could potentially be freed if you call SDL_SetStringProperty() or SDL_ClearProperty() on these properties from another thread. If you need to avoid this, use SDL_LockProperties() and SDL_UnlockProperties(). @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPropertyType @sa SDL_HasProperty @sa SDL_SetStringProperty<br>`props`：the properties to query.<br>`name`：the name of the property to query.<br>`default_value`：the default value of the property.<br>返回：the value of the property, or `default_value` if it is not set or not a string property. |
| <a id="api-103afe84151da5f2"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSurfaceAlphaMod(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, byte* alpha)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26862) | Get the additional alpha value used in blit operations. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetSurfaceColorMod @sa SDL_SetSurfaceAlphaMod<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to query.<br>`alpha`：a pointer filled in with the current alpha value.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a62561de12c3a18f"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSurfaceBlendMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, uint* blendMode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24068) | Get the blend mode used for blit operations. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetSurfaceBlendMode<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to query.<br>`blendMode`：a pointer filled in with the current SDL_BlendMode.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d03b48c4176bf308"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSurfaceClipRect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29760) | Get the clipping rectangle for a surface. When `surface` is the destination of a blit, only the area within the clip rectangle is drawn into. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetSurfaceClipRect<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure representing the surface to be clipped.<br>`rect`：an SDL_Rect structure filled in with the clipping rectangle for the surface.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-318d9b91d6f0c082"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSurfaceColorKey(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, uint* key)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20678) | Get the color key (transparent pixel) for a surface. The color key is a pixel of the format used by the surface, as generated by SDL_MapRGB(). If the surface doesn't have color key enabled this function returns false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetSurfaceColorKey @sa SDL_SurfaceHasColorKey<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to query.<br>`key`：a pointer filled in with the transparent pixel.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4f4b6785f7055899"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSurfaceColorMod(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, byte* r, byte* g, byte* b)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30011) | Get the additional color value multiplied into blit operations. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetSurfaceAlphaMod @sa SDL_SetSurfaceColorMod<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to query.<br>`r`：a pointer filled in with the current red color value.<br>`g`：a pointer filled in with the current green color value.<br>`b`：a pointer filled in with the current blue color value.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d213970600c85e6b"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLColorspace Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSurfaceColorspace(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44747) | Get the colorspace used by a surface. The colorspace defaults to SDL_COLORSPACE_SRGB_LINEAR for floating point formats, SDL_COLORSPACE_HDR10 for 10-bit formats, SDL_COLORSPACE_SRGB for other RGB surfaces and SDL_COLORSPACE_BT709_FULL for YUV textures. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetSurfaceColorspace<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to query.<br>返回：the colorspace used by the surface, or SDL_COLORSPACE_UNKNOWN if the surface is NULL. |
| <a id="api-dbc84b0cb4d179cf"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSurfaceImages(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50414) | Get an array including all versions of a surface. This returns all versions of a surface, with the surface being queried as the first element in the returned array. Freeing the array of surfaces does not affect the surfaces in the array. They are still referenced by the surface being queried and will be cleaned up normally. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AddSurfaceAlternateImage @sa SDL_RemoveSurfaceAlternateImages @sa SDL_SurfaceHasAlternateImages<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to query.<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of surface pointers returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a NULL terminated array of SDL_Surface pointers or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-520b5020ae573650"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPalette* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSurfacePalette(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36381) | Get the palette used by a surface. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetSurfacePalette<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to query.<br>返回：a pointer to the palette used by the surface, or NULL if there is no palette used. |
| <a id="api-3ebf6ceaa48df3bd"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSurfaceProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49134) | Get the properties associated with a surface. The following properties are understood by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_SURFACE_SDR_WHITE_POINT_FLOAT`: for HDR10 and floating point surfaces, this defines the value of 100% diffuse white, with higher values being displayed in the High Dynamic Range headroom. This defaults to 203 for HDR10 surfaces and 1.0 for floating point surfaces. - `SDL_PROP_SURFACE_HDR_HEADROOM_FLOAT`: for HDR10 and floating point surfaces, this defines the maximum dynamic range used by the content, in terms of the SDR white point. This defaults to 0.0, which disables tone mapping. - `SDL_PROP_SURFACE_TONEMAP_OPERATOR_STRING`: the tone mapping operator used when compressing from a surface with high dynamic range to another with lower dynamic range. Currently this supports "chrome", which uses the same tone mapping that Chrome uses for HDR content, the form "*=N", where N is a floating point scale factor applied in linear space, and "none", which disables tone mapping. This defaults to "chrome". - `SDL_PROP_SURFACE_HOTSPOT_X_NUMBER`: the hotspot pixel offset from the left edge of the image, if this surface is being used as a cursor. - `SDL_PROP_SURFACE_HOTSPOT_Y_NUMBER`: the hotspot pixel offset from the top edge of the image, if this surface is being used as a cursor. - `SDL_PROP_SURFACE_ROTATION_FLOAT`: the number of degrees a surface's data is meant to be rotated clockwise to make the image right-side up. Default 0. This is used by the camera API, if a mobile device is oriented differently than what its camera provides (i.e. - the camera always provides portrait images but the phone is being held in landscape orientation). Since SDL 3.4.0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to query.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8eaf7f7a8402d96a"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSystemPageSize()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33562) | Report the size of a page of memory. Different platforms might have different memory page sizes. In current times, 4 kilobytes is not unusual, but newer systems are moving to larger page sizes, and esoteric platforms might have any unexpected size. Note that this function can return 0, which means SDL can't determine the page size on this platform. It will _not_ set an error string to be retrieved with SDL_GetError() in this case! In this case, defaulting to 4096 is often a reasonable option. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>返回：the size of a single page of memory, in bytes, or 0 if SDL can't determine this information. |
| <a id="api-1205459a07ac9361"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSystemRam()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51197) | Get the amount of RAM configured in the system. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the amount of RAM configured in the system in MiB. |
| <a id="api-ba47f675374a9819"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSystemTheme Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetSystemTheme()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40929) | Get the current system theme. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the current system theme, light, dark, or unknown. |
| <a id="api-1d15a37b99279094"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTextInputArea(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect, int* cursor)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36754) | Get the area used to type Unicode text input. This returns the values previously set by SDL_SetTextInputArea(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetTextInputArea<br>`window`：the window for which to query the text input area.<br>`rect`：a pointer to an SDL_Rect filled in with the text input area, may be NULL.<br>`cursor`：a pointer to the offset of the current cursor location relative to `rect->x`, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-93c9d32b060f5eff"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTextureAlphaMod(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, byte* alpha)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36247) | Get the additional alpha value multiplied into render copy operations. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTextureAlphaModFloat @sa SDL_GetTextureColorMod @sa SDL_SetTextureAlphaMod<br>`texture`：the texture to query.<br>`alpha`：a pointer filled in with the current alpha value.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-588ea5fc7e0ef217"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTextureAlphaModFloat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, float* alpha)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42186) | Get the additional alpha value multiplied into render copy operations. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTextureAlphaMod @sa SDL_GetTextureColorModFloat @sa SDL_SetTextureAlphaModFloat<br>`texture`：the texture to query.<br>`alpha`：a pointer filled in with the current alpha value.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-84047023d472c370"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTextureBlendMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, uint* blendMode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20232) | Get the blend mode used for texture copy operations. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetTextureBlendMode<br>`texture`：the texture to query.<br>`blendMode`：a pointer filled in with the current SDL_BlendMode.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-16abeed21efcc171"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTextureColorMod(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, byte* r, byte* g, byte* b)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49193) | Get the additional color value multiplied into render copy operations. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTextureAlphaMod @sa SDL_GetTextureColorModFloat @sa SDL_SetTextureColorMod<br>`texture`：the texture to query.<br>`r`：a pointer filled in with the current red color value.<br>`g`：a pointer filled in with the current green color value.<br>`b`：a pointer filled in with the current blue color value.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7a628345dfeab08f"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTextureColorModFloat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, float* r, float* g, float* b)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48101) | Get the additional color value multiplied into render copy operations. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTextureAlphaModFloat @sa SDL_GetTextureColorMod @sa SDL_SetTextureColorModFloat<br>`texture`：the texture to query.<br>`r`：a pointer filled in with the current red color value.<br>`g`：a pointer filled in with the current green color value.<br>`b`：a pointer filled in with the current blue color value.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d3716ea88e489cb1"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPalette* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTexturePalette(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29221) | Get the palette used by a texture. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_SetTexturePalette<br>`texture`：the texture to query.<br>返回：a pointer to the palette used by the texture, or NULL if there is no palette used. |
| <a id="api-b3ee7607eef19001"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTextureProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50325) | Get the properties associated with a texture. The following read-only properties are provided by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_COLORSPACE_NUMBER`: an SDL_Colorspace value describing the texture colorspace. - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_FORMAT_NUMBER`: one of the enumerated values in SDL_PixelFormat. - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_ACCESS_NUMBER`: one of the enumerated values in SDL_TextureAccess. - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_WIDTH_NUMBER`: the width of the texture in pixels. - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_HEIGHT_NUMBER`: the height of the texture in pixels. - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_SDR_WHITE_POINT_FLOAT`: for HDR10 and floating point textures, this defines the value of 100% diffuse white, with higher values being displayed in the High Dynamic Range headroom. This defaults to 100 for HDR10 textures and 1.0 for other textures. - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_HDR_HEADROOM_FLOAT`: for HDR10 and floating point textures, this defines the maximum dynamic range used by the content, in terms of the SDR white point. If this is defined, any values outside the range supported by the display will be scaled into the available HDR headroom, otherwise they are clipped. This defaults to 1.0 for SDR textures, 4.0 for HDR10 textures, and no default for floating point textures. With the direct3d11 renderer: - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_D3D11_TEXTURE_POINTER`: the ID3D11Texture2D associated with the texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_D3D11_TEXTURE_U_POINTER`: the ID3D11Texture2D associated with the U plane of a YUV texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_D3D11_TEXTURE_V_POINTER`: the ID3D11Texture2D associated with the V plane of a YUV texture With the direct3d12 renderer: - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_D3D12_TEXTURE_POINTER`: the ID3D12Resource associated with the texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_D3D12_TEXTURE_U_POINTER`: the ID3D12Resource associated with the U plane of a YUV texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_D3D12_TEXTURE_V_POINTER`: the ID3D12Resource associated with the V plane of a YUV texture With the vulkan renderer: - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_VULKAN_TEXTURE_NUMBER`: the VkImage associated with the texture With the opengl renderer: - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGL_TEXTURE_NUMBER`: the GLuint texture associated with the texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGL_TEXTURE_UV_NUMBER`: the GLuint texture associated with the UV plane of an NV12 texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGL_TEXTURE_U_NUMBER`: the GLuint texture associated with the U plane of a YUV texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGL_TEXTURE_V_NUMBER`: the GLuint texture associated with the V plane of a YUV texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGL_TEXTURE_TARGET_NUMBER`: the GLenum for the texture target (`GL_TEXTURE_2D`, `GL_TEXTURE_RECTANGLE_ARB`, etc) - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGL_TEX_W_FLOAT`: the texture coordinate width of the texture (0.0 - 1.0) - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGL_TEX_H_FLOAT`: the texture coordinate height of the texture (0.0 - 1.0) With the opengles2 renderer: - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGLES2_TEXTURE_NUMBER`: the GLuint texture associated with the texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGLES2_TEXTURE_UV_NUMBER`: the GLuint texture associated with the UV plane of an NV12 texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGLES2_TEXTURE_U_NUMBER`: the GLuint texture associated with the U plane of a YUV texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGLES2_TEXTURE_V_NUMBER`: the GLuint texture associated with the V plane of a YUV texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_OPENGLES2_TEXTURE_TARGET_NUMBER`: the GLenum for the texture target (`GL_TEXTURE_2D`, `GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES`, etc) With the gpu renderer: - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_GPU_TEXTURE_POINTER`: the SDL_GPUTexture associated with the texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_GPU_TEXTURE_UV_POINTER`: the SDL_GPUTexture associated with the UV plane of an NV12 texture - `SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_GPU_TEXTURE_U_POINTER`: the SDL_GPUTexture associated with the U plane of a YUV texture -
