`SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_GPU_TEXTURE_V_POINTER`: the SDL_GPUTexture associated with the V plane of a YUV texture @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`texture`：the texture to query.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-cbd4667700513b31"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTextureScaleMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScaleMode* scaleMode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32123) | Get the scale mode used for texture scale operations. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetTextureScaleMode<br>`texture`：the texture to query.<br>`scaleMode`：a pointer filled in with the current scale mode.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b2484b5106b39e87"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTextureSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, float* w, float* h)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48452) | Get the size of a texture, as floating point values. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`texture`：the texture to query.<br>`w`：a pointer filled in with the width of the texture in pixels. This argument can be NULL if you don't need this information.<br>`h`：a pointer filled in with the height of the texture in pixels. This argument can be NULL if you don't need this information.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7999a15aae92844b"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetThreadId(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLThread* thread)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47123) | Get the thread identifier for the specified thread. This thread identifier is as reported by the underlying operating system. If SDL is running on a platform that does not support threads the return value will always be zero. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCurrentThreadID<br>`thread`：the thread to query.<br>返回：the ID of the specified thread, or the ID of the current thread if `thread` is NULL. |
| <a id="api-2b9d53f41f5be443"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetThreadName(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLThread* thread)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24366) | Get the thread name as it was specified in SDL_CreateThread(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`thread`：the thread to query.<br>返回：a pointer to a UTF-8 string that names the specified thread, or NULL if it doesn't have a name. |
| <a id="api-326ad6d250bc609d"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLThreadState Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetThreadState(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLThread* thread)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45329) | Get the current state of a thread. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ThreadState<br>`thread`：the thread to query.<br>返回：the current state of a thread, or SDL_THREAD_UNKNOWN if the thread isn't valid. |
| <a id="api-a07065bef14597c0"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTicks()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19476) | Get the number of milliseconds that have elapsed since the SDL library initialization. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTicksNS<br>返回：an unsigned 64‑bit integer that represents the number of milliseconds that have elapsed since the SDL library was initialized (typically via a call to SDL_Init). |
| <a id="api-066336400271456d"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTicksNs()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39374) | Get the number of nanoseconds since SDL library initialization. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：an unsigned 64-bit value representing the number of nanoseconds since the SDL library initialized. |
| <a id="api-a722beaae823ea7d"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTls(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAtomicInt* id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36724) | Get the current thread's value associated with a thread local storage ID. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetTLS<br>`id`：a pointer to the thread local storage ID, may not be NULL.<br>返回：the value associated with the ID for the current thread or NULL if no value has been set; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-79174d27d02980d5"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTouchDeviceName(ulong touchID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26490) | Get the touch device name as reported from the driver. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`touchID`：the touch device instance ID.<br>返回：touch device name, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7c23203508dc17fa"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTouchDeviceType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTouchDeviceType(ulong touchID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29306) | Get the type of the given touch device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`touchID`：the ID of a touch device.<br>返回：touch device type. |
| <a id="api-2029919cddef3dc5"></a>[`public ulong* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTouchDevices(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35100) | Get a list of registered touch devices. On some platforms SDL first sees the touch device if it was actually used. Therefore the returned list might be empty, although devices are available. After using all devices at least once the number will be correct. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of devices returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a 0 terminated array of touch device IDs or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-a45913f6378674ef"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFinger** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTouchFingers(ulong touchID, int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30311) | Get a list of active fingers for a given touch device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`touchID`：the ID of a touch device.<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of fingers returned, can be NULL.<br>返回：a NULL terminated array of SDL_Finger pointers or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This is a single allocation that should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-28e719f3ed4e9018"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTrayEntries(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayMenu* menu, int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20486) | Returns a list of entries in the menu, in order. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RemoveTrayEntry @sa SDL_InsertTrayEntryAt<br>`menu`：The menu to get entries from.<br>`count`：An optional pointer to obtain the number of entries in the menu.<br>返回：a NULL-terminated list of entries within the given menu. The pointer becomes invalid when any function that inserts or deletes entries in the menu is called. |
| <a id="api-0d8fcfa3a4075765"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTrayEntryChecked(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry* entry)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38790) | Gets whether or not an entry is checked. The entry must have been created with the SDL_TRAYENTRY_CHECKBOX flag. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTrayEntries @sa SDL_InsertTrayEntryAt @sa SDL_SetTrayEntryChecked<br>`entry`：the entry to be read.<br>返回：true if the entry is checked; false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-063288aeebb4686e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTrayEntryEnabled(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry* entry)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38201) | Gets whether or not an entry is enabled. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTrayEntries @sa SDL_InsertTrayEntryAt @sa SDL_SetTrayEntryEnabled<br>`entry`：the entry to be read.<br>返回：true if the entry is enabled; false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-accdae845cf772c0"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTrayEntryLabel(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry* entry)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43081) | Gets the label of an entry. If the returned value is NULL, the entry is a separator. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTrayEntries @sa SDL_InsertTrayEntryAt @sa SDL_SetTrayEntryLabel<br>`entry`：the entry to be read.<br>返回：the label of the entry in UTF-8 encoding. |
| <a id="api-2b1dd532caa49a67"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayMenu* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTrayEntryParent(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry* entry)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18703) | Gets the menu containing a certain tray entry. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_InsertTrayEntryAt<br>`entry`：the entry for which to get the parent menu.<br>返回：the parent menu. |
| <a id="api-9715e36191840e26"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayMenu* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTrayMenu(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTray* tray)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44239) | Gets a previously created tray menu. You should have called SDL_CreateTrayMenu() on the tray object. This function allows you to fetch it again later. This function does the same thing as SDL_GetTraySubmenu(), except that it takes a SDL_Tray instead of a SDL_TrayEntry. A menu does not need to be destroyed; it will be destroyed with the tray. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateTray @sa SDL_CreateTrayMenu<br>`tray`：the tray entry to bind the menu to.<br>返回：the newly created menu. |
| <a id="api-61644c8ec6a475f7"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTrayMenuParentEntry(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayMenu* menu)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17961) | Gets the entry for which the menu is a submenu, if the current menu is a submenu. Either this function or SDL_GetTrayMenuParentTray() will return non-NULL for any given menu. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateTraySubmenu @sa SDL_GetTrayMenuParentTray<br>`menu`：the menu for which to get the parent entry.<br>返回：the parent entry, or NULL if this menu is not a submenu. |
| <a id="api-4c15714f0061a924"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTray* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTrayMenuParentTray(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayMenu* menu)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30842) | Gets the tray for which this menu is the first-level menu, if the current menu isn't a submenu. Either this function or SDL_GetTrayMenuParentEntry() will return non-NULL for any given menu. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateTrayMenu @sa SDL_GetTrayMenuParentEntry<br>`menu`：the menu for which to get the parent enttrayry.<br>返回：the parent tray, or NULL if this menu is a submenu. |
| <a id="api-dc0388f3121734bc"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayMenu* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetTraySubmenu(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry* entry)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45236) | Gets a previously created tray entry submenu. You should have called SDL_CreateTraySubmenu() on the entry object. This function allows you to fetch it again later. This function does the same thing as SDL_GetTrayMenu(), except that it takes a SDL_TrayEntry instead of a SDL_Tray. A menu does not need to be destroyed; it will be destroyed with the tray. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_InsertTrayEntryAt @sa SDL_CreateTraySubmenu<br>`entry`：the tray entry to bind the menu to.<br>返回：the newly created menu. |
| <a id="api-c722b9d5c5c4c850"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetUserFolder(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFolder folder)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33954) | Finds the most suitable user folder for a specific purpose. Many OSes provide certain standard folders for certain purposes, such as storing pictures, music or videos for a certain user. This function gives the path for many of those special locations. This function is specifically for _user_ folders, which are meant for the user to access and manage. For application-specific folders, meant to hold data for the application to manage, see SDL_GetBasePath() and SDL_GetPrefPath(). The returned path is guaranteed to end with a path separator (' \ ' on Windows, '/' on most other platforms). If NULL is returned, the error may be obtained with SDL_GetError(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`folder`：the type of folder to find.<br>返回：either a null-terminated C string containing the full path to the folder, or NULL if an error happened. |
| <a id="api-8ff85d105c998177"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetVersion()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36703) | Get the version of SDL that is linked against your program. If you are linking to SDL dynamically, then it is possible that the current version will be different than the version you compiled against. This function returns the current version, while SDL_VERSION is the version you compiled with. This function may be called safely at any time, even before SDL_Init(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRevision<br>返回：the version of the linked library. |
| <a id="api-8584e1d19366ce70"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetVideoDriver(int index)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51337) | Get the name of a built in video driver. The video drivers are presented in the order in which they are normally checked during initialization. The names of drivers are all simple, low-ASCII identifiers, like "cocoa", "x11" or "windows". These never have Unicode characters, and are not meant to be proper names. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumVideoDrivers<br>`index`：the index of a video driver.<br>返回：the name of the video driver with the given **index**, or NULL if index is out of bounds. |
| <a id="api-a6abf433decc6ba0"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowAspectRatio(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, float* min_aspect, float* max_aspect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37603) | Get the aspect ratio of a window's client area. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowAspectRatio<br>`window`：the window to query the width and height from.<br>`min_aspect`：a pointer filled in with the minimum aspect ratio of the window, may be NULL.<br>`max_aspect`：a pointer filled in with the maximum aspect ratio of the window, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-2f78b99b0dd731b0"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowBordersSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int* top, int* left, int* bottom, int* right)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38608) | Get the size of a window's borders (decorations) around the client area. Note: If this function fails (returns false), the size values will be initialized to 0, 0, 0, 0 (if a non-NULL pointer is provided), as if the window in question was borderless. Note: This function may fail on systems where the window has not yet been decorated by the display server (for example, immediately after calling SDL_CreateWindow). It is recommended that you wait at least until the window has been presented and composited, so that the window system has a chance to decorate the window and provide the border dimensions to SDL. This function also returns false if getting the information is not supported. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowSize<br>`window`：the window to query the size values of the border (decorations) from.<br>`top`：pointer to variable for storing the size of the top border; NULL is permitted.<br>`left`：pointer to variable for storing the size of the left border; NULL is permitted.<br>`bottom`：pointer to variable for storing the size of the bottom border; NULL is permitted.<br>`right`：pointer to variable for storing the size of the right border; NULL is permitted.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a7be328891759f9e"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowDisplayScale(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52313) | Get the content display scale relative to a window's pixel size. This is a combination of the window pixel density and the display content scale, and is the expected scale for displaying content in this window. For example, if a 3840x2160 window had a display scale of 2.0, the user expects the content to take twice as many pixels and be the same physical size as if it were being displayed in a 1920x1080 window with a display scale of 1.0. Conceptually this value corresponds to the scale display setting, and is updated when that setting is changed, or the window moves to a display with a different scale setting. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：the display scale, or 0.0f on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-808a708663a63a0c"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowFlags(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33101) | Get the window flags. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateWindow @sa SDL_HideWindow @sa SDL_MaximizeWindow @sa SDL_MinimizeWindow @sa SDL_SetWindowFullscreen @sa SDL_SetWindowMouseGrab @sa SDL_SetWindowFillDocument @sa SDL_ShowWindow<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：a mask of the SDL_WindowFlags associated with `window`. |
| <a id="api-e45f98a150764586"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowFromEvent(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEvent* @event)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35275) | Get window associated with an event. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PollEvent @sa SDL_WaitEvent @sa SDL_WaitEventTimeout<br>`event`：an event containing a `windowID`.<br>返回：the associated window on success or NULL if there is none. |
| <a id="api-ab9ace9703085ba5"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowFromId(uint id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17669) | Get a window from a stored ID. The numeric ID is what SDL_WindowEvent references, and is necessary to map these events to specific SDL_Window objects. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowID<br>`id`：the ID of the window.<br>返回：the window associated with `id` or NULL if it doesn't exist; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-52e89e2e7c7e1556"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayMode* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowFullscreenMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46637) | Query the display mode to use when a window is visible at fullscreen. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowFullscreenMode @sa SDL_SetWindowFullscreen<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：a pointer to the exclusive fullscreen mode to use or NULL for borderless fullscreen desktop mode. |
| <a id="api-6910b66e9089c416"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowIccProfile(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, ulong* size)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33261) | Get the raw ICC profile data for the screen the window is currently on. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>`size`：the size of the ICC profile.<br>返回：the raw ICC profile data on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-32b0c6852843bb81"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowId(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31550) | Get the numeric ID of a window. The numeric ID is what SDL_WindowEvent references, and is necessary to map these events to specific SDL_Window objects. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowFromID<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：the ID of the window on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-73a4ffc5ee46c07d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowKeyboardGrab(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22661) | Get a window's keyboard grab mode. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowKeyboardGrab<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：true if keyboard is grabbed, and false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-fbcfb16e8c382540"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowMaximumSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int* w, int* h)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27852) | Get the maximum size of a window's client area. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowMinimumSize @sa SDL_SetWindowMaximumSize<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>`w`：a pointer filled in with the maximum width of the window, may be NULL.<br>`h`：a pointer filled in with the maximum height of the window, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7a9093fea793f752"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowMinimumSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int* w, int* h)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24627) | Get the minimum size of a window's client area. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowMaximumSize @sa SDL_SetWindowMinimumSize<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>`w`：a pointer filled in with the minimum width of the window, may be NULL.<br>`h`：a pointer filled in with the minimum height of the window, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c1cb82d3068ea343"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowMouseGrab(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34006) | Get a window's mouse grab mode. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowMouseRect @sa SDL_SetWindowMouseRect @sa SDL_SetWindowMouseGrab @sa SDL_SetWindowKeyboardGrab<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：true if mouse is grabbed, and false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-8531f5e2ba1fff53"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowMouseRect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29244) | Get the mouse confinement rectangle of a window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowMouseRect @sa SDL_GetWindowMouseGrab @sa SDL_SetWindowMouseGrab<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：a pointer to the mouse confinement rectangle of a window, or NULL if there isn't one. |
| <a id="api-fff942b05c70e94f"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowOpacity(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34890) | Get the opacity of a window. If transparency isn't supported on this platform, opacity will be returned as 1.0f without error. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowOpacity<br>`window`：the window to get the current opacity value from.<br>返回：the opacity, (0.0f - transparent, 1.0f - opaque), or -1.0f on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-41610b00c407a81f"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowParent(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16851) | Get parent of a window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreatePopupWindow<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：the parent of the window on success or NULL if the window has no parent. |
| <a id="api-c00099b23bc6c0b9"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowPixelDensity(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21381) | Get the pixel density of a window. This is a ratio of pixel size to window size. For example, if the window is 1920x1080 and it has a high density back buffer of 3840x2160 pixels, it would have a pixel density of 2.0. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowDisplayScale<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：the pixel density or 0.0f on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-18a42753e630b259"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowPixelFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48473) | Get the pixel format associated with the window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：the pixel format of the window on success or SDL_PIXELFORMAT_UNKNOWN on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-53ae462eca801bca"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowPosition(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int* x, int* y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43168) | Get the position of a window. This is the current position of the window as last reported by the windowing system. If you do not need the value for one of the positions a NULL may be passed in the `x` or `y` parameter. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowPosition<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>`x`：a pointer filled in with the x position of the window, may be NULL.<br>`y`：a pointer filled in with the y position of the window, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8b9464187847abea"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLProgressState Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowProgressState(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20912) | Get the state of the progress bar for the given window’s taskbar icon. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`window`：the window to get the current progress state from.<br>返回：the progress state, or `SDL_PROGRESS_STATE_INVALID` on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-75c9f4c8589a58ec"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowProgressValue(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36543) | Get the value of the progress bar for the given window’s taskbar icon. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`window`：the window to get the current progress value from.<br>返回：the progress value in the range of [0.0f - 1.0f], or -1.0f on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3e7f1db82a183fe7"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22425) | Get the properties associated with a window. The following read-only properties are provided by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_SHAPE_POINTER`: the surface associated with a shaped window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_HDR_ENABLED_BOOLEAN`: true if the window has HDR headroom above the SDR white point. This property can change dynamically when SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_HDR_STATE_CHANGED is sent. - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_SDR_WHITE_LEVEL_FLOAT`: the value of SDR white in the SDL_COLORSPACE_SRGB_LINEAR colorspace. On Windows this corresponds to the SDR white level in scRGB colorspace, and on Apple platforms this is always 1.0 for EDR content. This property can change dynamically when SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_HDR_STATE_CHANGED is sent. - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_HDR_HEADROOM_FLOAT`: the additional high dynamic range that can be displayed, in terms of the SDR white point. When HDR is not enabled, this will be 1.0. This property can change dynamically when SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_HDR_STATE_CHANGED is sent. On Android: - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_ANDROID_WINDOW_POINTER`: the ANativeWindow associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_ANDROID_SURFACE_POINTER`: the EGLSurface associated with the window On iOS: - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_UIKIT_WINDOW_POINTER`: the `(__unsafe_unretained)` UIWindow associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_UIKIT_METAL_VIEW_TAG_NUMBER`: the NSInteger tag associated with metal views on the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_UIKIT_OPENGL_FRAMEBUFFER_NUMBER`: the OpenGL view's framebuffer object. It must be bound when rendering to the screen using OpenGL. - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_UIKIT_OPENGL_RENDERBUFFER_NUMBER`: the OpenGL view's renderbuffer object. It must be bound when SDL_GL_SwapWindow is called. - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_UIKIT_OPENGL_RESOLVE_FRAMEBUFFER_NUMBER`: the OpenGL view's resolve framebuffer, when MSAA is used. On KMS/DRM: - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_KMSDRM_DEVICE_INDEX_NUMBER`: the device index associated with the window (e.g. the X in /dev/dri/cardX) - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_KMSDRM_DRM_FD_NUMBER`: the DRM FD associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_KMSDRM_GBM_DEVICE_POINTER`: the GBM device associated with the window On macOS: - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_COCOA_WINDOW_POINTER`: the `(__unsafe_unretained)` NSWindow associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_COCOA_METAL_VIEW_TAG_NUMBER`: the NSInteger tag associated with metal views on the window On OpenVR: - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_OPENVR_OVERLAY_ID_NUMBER`: the OpenVR Overlay Handle ID for the associated overlay window. On QNX: - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_QNX_WINDOW_POINTER`: the screen_window_t associated with the window. - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_QNX_SURFACE_POINTER`: the EGLSurface associated with the window On Vivante: - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_VIVANTE_DISPLAY_POINTER`: the EGLNativeDisplayType associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_VIVANTE_WINDOW_POINTER`: the EGLNativeWindowType associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_VIVANTE_SURFACE_POINTER`: the EGLSurface associated with the window On Windows: - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WIN32_HWND_POINTER`: the HWND associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WIN32_HDC_POINTER`: the HDC associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WIN32_INSTANCE_POINTER`: the HINSTANCE associated with the window On Wayland: Note: The `xdg_*` window objects do not internally persist across window show/hide calls. They will be null if the window is hidden and must be queried each time it is shown. - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WAYLAND_DISPLAY_POINTER`: the wl_display associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WAYLAND_SURFACE_POINTER`: the wl_surface associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WAYLAND_VIEWPORT_POINTER`: the wp_viewport associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WAYLAND_EGL_WINDOW_POINTER`: the wl_egl_window associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WAYLAND_XDG_SURFACE_POINTER`: the xdg_surface associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WAYLAND_XDG_TOPLEVEL_POINTER`: the xdg_toplevel role associated with the window - 'SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WAYLAND_XDG_TOPLEVEL_EXPORT_HANDLE_STRING': the export handle associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WAYLAND_XDG_POPUP_POINTER`: the xdg_popup role associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_WAYLAND_XDG_POSITIONER_POINTER`: the xdg_positioner associated with the window, in popup mode On X11: - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_X11_DISPLAY_POINTER`: the X11 Display associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_X11_SCREEN_NUMBER`: the screen number associated with the window - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_X11_WINDOW_NUMBER`: the X11 Window associated with the window On Emscripten: - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_EMSCRIPTEN_CANVAS_ID_STRING`: the id the canvas element will have - `SDL_PROP_WINDOW_EMSCRIPTEN_KEYBOARD_ELEMENT_STRING`: the keyboard element that associates keyboard events to this window @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0afc96d678716e35"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowRelativeMouseMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24044) | Query whether relative mouse mode is enabled for a window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowRelativeMouseMode<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：true if relative mode is enabled for a window or false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-fe395191a4ae7bae"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowSafeArea(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33725) | Get the safe area for this window. Some devices have portions of the screen which are partially obscured or not interactive, possibly due to on-screen controls, curved edges, camera notches, TV overscan, etc. This function provides the area of the window which is safe to have interactable content. You should continue rendering into the rest of the window, but it should not contain visually important or interactable content. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>`rect`：a pointer filled in with the client area that is safe for interactive content.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0e2c1c5da0bba729"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int* w, int* h)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26712) | Get the size of a window's client area. The window pixel size may differ from its window coordinate size if the window is on a high pixel density display. Use SDL_GetWindowSizeInPixels() or SDL_GetRenderOutputSize() to get the real client area size in pixels. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderOutputSize @sa SDL_GetWindowSizeInPixels @sa SDL_SetWindowSize @sa SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_RESIZED<br>`window`：the window to query the width and height from.<br>`w`：a pointer filled in with the width of the window, may be NULL.<br>`h`：a pointer filled in with the height of the window, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-319626e93452c21c"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowSizeInPixels(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int* w, int* h)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45127) | Get the size of a window's client area, in pixels. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateWindow @sa SDL_GetWindowSize<br>`window`：the window from which the drawable size should be queried.<br>`w`：a pointer to variable for storing the width in pixels, may be NULL.<br>`h`：a pointer to variable for storing the height in pixels, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0b875fd58f79cc76"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowSurface(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18385) | Get the SDL surface associated with the window. A new surface will be created with the optimal format for the window, if necessary. This surface will be freed when the window is destroyed. Do not free this surface. This surface will be invalidated if the window is resized. After resizing a window this function must be called again to return a valid surface. You may not combine this with 3D or the rendering API on this window. This function is affected by `SDL_HINT_FRAMEBUFFER_ACCELERATION`. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_DestroyWindowSurface @sa SDL_WindowHasSurface @sa SDL_UpdateWindowSurface @sa SDL_UpdateWindowSurfaceRects<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：the surface associated with the window, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c5d99946b463b321"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowSurfaceVSync(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int* vsync)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18248) | Get VSync for the window surface. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowSurfaceVSync<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>`vsync`：an int filled with the current vertical refresh sync interval. See SDL_SetWindowSurfaceVSync() for the meaning of the value.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-90ec1aa3b61e1634"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindowTitle(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19220) | Get the title of a window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowTitle<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：the title of the window in UTF-8 format or "" if there is no title. |
| <a id="api-ccc2e60a091e1f59"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetWindows(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26047) | Get a list of valid windows. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of windows returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a NULL terminated array of SDL_Window pointers or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This is a single allocation that should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-3feaf80c8d756325"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Getenv(sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22795) | Get the value of a variable in the environment. The name of the variable is case sensitive on all platforms. This function uses SDL's cached copy of the environment and is thread-safe. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`name`：the name of the variable to get.<br>返回：a pointer to the value of the variable or NULL if it can't be found. |
| <a id="api-6cf50fb94ec4182a"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetenvUnsafe(sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31920) | Get the value of a variable in the environment. This function bypasses SDL's cached copy of the environment and is not thread-safe. On some platforms, this may make case-insensitive matches, while other platforms are case-sensitive. It is best to be precise with strings used for queries through this interface. SDL_getenv is always case-sensitive, however. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe, consider using SDL_getenv() instead. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_getenv<br>`name`：the name of the variable to get.<br>返回：a pointer to the value of the variable or NULL if it can't be found. |
| <a id="api-c98bc520a0afaa56"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGLContextState* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlCreateContext(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21259) | Create an OpenGL context for an OpenGL window, and make it current. The OpenGL context will be created with the current states set through SDL_GL_SetAttribute(). The SDL_Window specified must have been created with the SDL_WINDOW_OPENGL flag, or context creation will fail. Windows users new to OpenGL should note that, for historical reasons, GL functions added after OpenGL version 1.1 are not available by default. Those functions must be loaded at run-time, either with an OpenGL extension-handling library or with SDL_GL_GetProcAddress() and its related functions. SDL_GLContext is opaque to the application. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_DestroyContext @sa SDL_GL_MakeCurrent<br>`window`：the window to associate with the context.<br>返回：the OpenGL context associated with `window` or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-53bbabba2bdabed1"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlDestroyContext(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGLContextState* context)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51965) | Delete an OpenGL context. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_CreateContext<br>`context`：the OpenGL context to be deleted.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-60d949d290d45fe0"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlExtensionSupported(sbyte* extension)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41129) | Check if an OpenGL extension is supported for the current context. This function operates on the current GL context; you must have created a context and it must be current before calling this function. Do not assume that all contexts you create will have the same set of extensions available, or that recreating an existing context will offer the same extensions again. While it's probably not a massive overhead, this function is not an O(1) operation. Check the extensions you care about after creating the GL context and save that information somewhere instead of calling the function every time you need to know. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`extension`：the name of the extension to check.<br>返回：true if the extension is supported, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-d9f3c4a0c65152cb"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlGetAttribute(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGLAttr attr, int* value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50585) | Get the actual value for an attribute from the current context. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_ResetAttributes @sa SDL_GL_SetAttribute<br>`attr`：an SDL_GLAttr enum value specifying the OpenGL attribute to get.<br>`value`：a pointer filled in with the current value of `attr`.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f90026d75d3e8c1f"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGLContextState* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlGetCurrentContext()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50119) | Get the currently active OpenGL context. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_MakeCurrent<br>返回：the currently active OpenGL context or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-838a1991e18420ed"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlGetCurrentWindow()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34773) | Get the currently active OpenGL window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the currently active OpenGL window on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b75e4015664bb3d1"></a>[`public delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void> Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlGetProcAddress(sbyte* proc)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41673) | Get an OpenGL function by name. If the GL library is loaded at runtime with SDL_GL_LoadLibrary(), then all GL functions must be retrieved this way. Usually this is used to retrieve function pointers to OpenGL extensions. There are some quirks to looking up OpenGL functions that require some extra care from the application. If you code carefully, you can handle these quirks without any platform-specific code, though: - On Windows, function pointers are specific to the current GL context; this means you need to have created a GL context and made it current before calling SDL_GL_GetProcAddress(). If you recreate your context or create a second context, you should assume that any existing function pointers aren't valid to use with it. This is (currently) a Windows-specific limitation, and in practice lots of drivers don't suffer this limitation, but it is still the way the wgl API is documented to work and you should expect crashes if you don't respect it. Store a copy of the function pointers that comes and goes with context lifespan. - On X11, function pointers returned by this function are valid for any context, and can even be looked up before a context is created at all. This means that, for at least some common OpenGL implementations, if you look up a function that doesn't exist, you'll get a non-NULL result that is _NOT_ safe to call. You must always make sure the function is actually available for a given GL context before calling it, by checking for the existence of the appropriate extension with SDL_GL_ExtensionSupported(), or verifying that the version of OpenGL you're using offers the function as core functionality. - Some OpenGL drivers, on all platforms, *will* return NULL if a function isn't supported, but you can't count on this behavior. Check for extensions you use, and if you get a NULL anyway, act as if that extension wasn't available. This is probably a bug in the driver, but you can code defensively for this scenario anyhow. - Just because you're on Linux/Unix, don't assume you'll be using X11. Next-gen display servers are waiting to replace it, and may or may not make the same promises about function pointers. - OpenGL function pointers must be declared `APIENTRY` as in the example code. This will ensure the proper calling convention is followed on platforms where this matters (Win32) thereby avoiding stack corruption. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_ExtensionSupported @sa SDL_GL_LoadLibrary @sa SDL_GL_UnloadLibrary<br>`proc`：the name of an OpenGL function.<br>返回：a pointer to the named OpenGL function. The returned pointer should be cast to the appropriate function signature. |
| <a id="api-dc924a2be5cad2b7"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlGetSwapInterval(int* interval)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17721) | Get the swap interval for the current OpenGL context. If the system can't determine the swap interval, or there isn't a valid current context, this function will set *interval to 0 as a safe default. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_SetSwapInterval<br>`interval`：output interval value. 0 if there is no vertical retrace synchronization, 1 if the buffer swap is synchronized with the vertical retrace, and -1 if late swaps happen immediately instead of waiting for the next retrace.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5d8414fdb673370b"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlLoadLibrary(sbyte* path)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21794) | Dynamically load an OpenGL library. This should be done after initializing the video driver, but before creating any OpenGL windows. If no OpenGL library is loaded, the default library will be loaded upon creation of the first OpenGL window. If you do this, you need to retrieve all of the GL functions used in your program from the dynamic library using SDL_GL_GetProcAddress(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_GetProcAddress @sa SDL_GL_UnloadLibrary<br>`path`：the platform dependent OpenGL library name, or NULL to open the default OpenGL library.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-88e464a48f4d0543"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlMakeCurrent(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGLContextState* context)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38226) | Set up an OpenGL context for rendering into an OpenGL window. The context must have been created with a compatible window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_CreateContext<br>`window`：the window to associate with the context.<br>`context`：the OpenGL context to associate with the window.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-2d3e89e96a8d8c26"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlResetAttributes()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42865) | Reset all previously set OpenGL context attributes to their default values. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_GetAttribute @sa SDL_GL_SetAttribute |
| <a id="api-d826e9796d0fda31"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlSetAttribute(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGLAttr attr, int value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32471) | Set an OpenGL window attribute before window creation. This function sets the OpenGL attribute `attr` to `value`. The requested attributes should be set before creating an OpenGL window. You should use SDL_GL_GetAttribute() to check the values after creating the OpenGL context, since the values obtained can differ from the requested ones. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_CreateContext @sa SDL_GL_GetAttribute @sa SDL_GL_ResetAttributes<br>`attr`：an enum value specifying the OpenGL attribute to set.<br>`value`：the desired value for the attribute.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-134f8c026ef6cd6f"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlSetSwapInterval(int interval)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18442) | Set the swap interval for the current OpenGL context. Some systems allow specifying -1 for the interval, to enable adaptive vsync. Adaptive vsync works the same as vsync, but if you've already missed the vertical retrace for a given frame, it swaps buffers immediately, which might be less jarring for the user during occasional framerate drops. If an application requests adaptive vsync and the system does not support it, this function will fail and return false. In such a case, you should probably retry the call with 1 for the interval. Adaptive vsync is implemented for some glX drivers with GLX_EXT_swap_control_tear, and for some Windows drivers with WGL_EXT_swap_control_tear. Read more on the Khronos wiki: https://www.khronos.org/opengl/wiki/Swap_Interval#Adaptive_Vsync @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_GetSwapInterval<br>`interval`：0 for immediate updates, 1 for updates synchronized with the vertical retrace, -1 for adaptive vsync.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-56630f0b489dee5d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlSwapWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21318) | Update a window with OpenGL rendering. This is used with double-buffered OpenGL contexts, which are the default. On macOS, make sure you bind 0 to the draw framebuffer before swapping the window, otherwise nothing will happen. If you aren't using glBindFramebuffer(), this is the default and you won't have to do anything extra. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window to change.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5ebb51c50d54bf2c"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlUnloadLibrary()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45250) | Unload the OpenGL library previously loaded by SDL_GL_LoadLibrary(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_LoadLibrary |
| <a id="api-19b0b076016a62ed"></a>[`public sbyte** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlobDirectory(sbyte* path, sbyte* pattern, uint flags, int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19437) | Enumerate a directory tree, filtered by pattern, and return a list. Files are filtered out if they don't match the string in `pattern`, which may contain wildcard characters `*` (match everything) and `?` (match one character). If pattern is NULL, no filtering is done and all results are returned. Subdirectories are permitted, and are specified with a path separator of `/`. Wildcard characters `*` and `?` never match a path separator. `flags` may be set to SDL_GLOB_CASEINSENSITIVE to make the pattern matching case-insensitive. The returned array is always NULL-terminated, for your iterating convenience, but if `count` is non-NULL, on return it will contain the number of items in the array, not counting the NULL terminator. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`path`：the path of the directory to enumerate.<br>`pattern`：the pattern that files in the directory must match. Can be NULL.<br>`flags`：`SDL_GLOB_*` bitflags that affect this search.<br>`count`：on return, will be set to the number of items in the returned array. Can be NULL.<br>返回：an array of strings on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This is a single allocation that should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-a2fda14582a9fa0b"></a>[`public sbyte** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GlobStorageDirectory(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLStorage* storage, sbyte* path, sbyte* pattern, uint flags, int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48178) | Enumerate a directory tree, filtered by pattern, and return a list. Files are filtered out if they don't match the string in `pattern`, which may contain wildcard characters `*` (match everything) and `?` (match one character). If pattern is NULL, no filtering is done and all results are returned. Subdirectories are permitted, and are specified with a path separator of '/'. Wildcard characters `*` and `?` never match a path separator. `flags` may be set to SDL_GLOB_CASEINSENSITIVE to make the pattern matching case-insensitive. The returned array is always NULL-terminated, for your iterating convenience, but if `count` is non-NULL, on return it will contain the number of items in the array, not counting the NULL terminator. If `path` is NULL, this is treated as a request to enumerate the root of the storage container's tree. An empty string also works for this. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, assuming the `storage` object is thread-safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`storage`：a storage container.<br>`path`：the path of the directory to enumerate, or NULL for the root.<br>`pattern`：the pattern that files in the directory must match. Can be NULL.<br>`flags`：`SDL_GLOB_*` bitflags that affect this search.<br>`count`：on return, will be set to the number of items in the returned array. Can be NULL.<br>返回：an array of strings on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. The caller should pass the returned pointer to SDL_free when done with it. This is a single allocation that should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-a85832ba871f683e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GpuSupportsProperties(uint props)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42041) | Checks for GPU runtime support. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateGPUDeviceWithProperties<br>`props`：the properties to use.<br>返回：true if supported, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-59789982f5117a09"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GpuSupportsShaderFormats(uint format_flags, sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46615) | Checks for GPU runtime support. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateGPUDevice<br>`format_flags`：a bitflag indicating which shader formats the app is able to provide.<br>`name`：the preferred GPU driver, or NULL to let SDL pick the optimal driver.<br>返回：true if supported, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-aa2619c156422f19"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GpuTextureFormatTexelBlockSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27197) | Obtains the texel block size for a texture format. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_UploadToGPUTexture<br>`format`：the texture format you want to know the texel size of.<br>返回：the texel block size of the texture format. |
| <a id="api-4302afb24aeb9a8b"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GpuTextureSupportsFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureFormat format, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureType type, uint usage)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49445) | Determines whether a texture format is supported for a given type and usage. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>`format`：the texture format to check.<br>`type`：the type of texture (2D, 3D, Cube).<br>`usage`：a bitmask of all usage scenarios to check.<br>返回：whether the texture format is supported for this type and usage. |
| <a id="api-6ebaca1af4cc313a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GpuTextureSupportsSampleCount(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureFormat format, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUSampleCount sample_count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40572) | Determines if a sample count for a texture format is supported. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>`format`：the texture format to check.<br>`sample_count`：the sample count to check.<br>返回：whether the sample count is supported for this texture format. |
| <a id="api-fa75827929d06ec7"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GuidToString(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGUID* guid, sbyte* pszGUID, int cbGUID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18656) | Get an ASCII string representation for a given SDL_GUID. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_StringToGUID<br>`guid`：the SDL_GUID you wish to convert to string.<br>`pszGUID`：buffer in which to write the ASCII string.<br>`cbGUID`：the size of pszGUID, should be at least 33 bytes. |
| <a id="api-6d2a75c933b69908"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HapticEffectSupported(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHapticEffect* effect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44892) | Check to see if an effect is supported by a haptic device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateHapticEffect @sa SDL_GetHapticFeatures<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to query.<br>`effect`：the desired effect to query.<br>返回：true if the effect is supported or false if it isn't. |
| <a id="api-4ab8391fa1c717c9"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HapticRumbleSupported(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48274) | Check whether rumble is supported on a haptic device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_InitHapticRumble<br>`haptic`：haptic device to check for rumble support.<br>返回：true if the effect is supported or false if it isn't. |
| <a id="api-4205a33a4e5c395b"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasAltiVec()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52871) | Determine whether the CPU has AltiVec features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using PowerPC instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true if the CPU has AltiVec features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-0671a4d90e936806"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasArmsimd()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50213) | Determine whether the CPU has ARM SIMD (ARMv6) features. This is different from ARM NEON, which is a different instruction set. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using ARM instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasNEON<br>返回：true if the CPU has ARM SIMD features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-b2f35298a03775ab"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasAvx()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30618) | Determine whether the CPU has AVX features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasAVX2 @sa SDL_HasAVX512F<br>返回：true if the CPU has AVX features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-aa04e51d2be326af"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasAvx2()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47780) | Determine whether the CPU has AVX2 features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasAVX @sa SDL_HasAVX512F<br>返回：true if the CPU has AVX2 features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-acb2478471ab7d3d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasAvx512F()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16720) | Determine whether the CPU has AVX-512F (foundation) features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasAVX @sa SDL_HasAVX2<br>返回：true if the CPU has AVX-512F features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-5188fd8bc520501a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasClipboardData(sbyte* mime_type)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29587) | Query whether there is data in the clipboard for the provided mime type. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetClipboardData @sa SDL_GetClipboardData<br>`mime_type`：the mime type to check for data.<br>返回：true if data exists in the clipboard for the provided mime type, false if it does not. |

If you need to check for a range of event types, use SDL_HasEvents() instead. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasEvents<br>`type`：the type of event to be queried; see SDL_EventType for details.<br>返回：true if events matching `type` are present, or false if events matching `type` are not present. |
| <a id="api-c6ab9bac6cb968b8"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasEvents(uint minType, uint maxType)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21850) | Check for the existence of certain event types in the event queue. If you need to check for a single event type, use SDL_HasEvent() instead. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasEvents<br>`minType`：the low end of event type to be queried, inclusive; see SDL_EventType for details.<br>`maxType`：the high end of event type to be queried, inclusive; see SDL_EventType for details.<br>返回：true if events with type >= `minType` and < = `maxType` are present, or false if not. |
| <a id="api-48a5d8eb2505561d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasGamepad()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30742) | Return whether a gamepad is currently connected. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>返回：true if a gamepad is connected, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-0a6ef121029d8911"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasJoystick()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37812) | Return whether a joystick is currently connected. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoysticks<br>返回：true if a joystick is connected, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-3889e8cdf64bf672"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasKeyboard()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28606) | Return whether a keyboard is currently connected. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyboards<br>返回：true if a keyboard is connected, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-ea1f066b2980e5ed"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasLasx()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26024) | Determine whether the CPU has LASX (LOONGARCH SIMD) features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using LOONGARCH instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true if the CPU has LOONGARCH LASX features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-f5f4b1f3295f37ce"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasLsx()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42914) | Determine whether the CPU has LSX (LOONGARCH SIMD) features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using LOONGARCH instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true if the CPU has LOONGARCH LSX features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-16a0907bc245ee79"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasMmx()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52909) | Determine whether the CPU has MMX features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true if the CPU has MMX features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-8feb8f71e0adc131"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasMouse()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48914) | Return whether a mouse is currently connected. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetMice<br>返回：true if a mouse is connected, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-87a077d54cc31469"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasNeon()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20723) | Determine whether the CPU has NEON (ARM SIMD) features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using ARM instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true if the CPU has ARM NEON features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-d4c270396435948c"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasPrimarySelectionText()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25345) | Query whether the primary selection exists and contains a non-empty text string. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPrimarySelectionText @sa SDL_SetPrimarySelectionText<br>返回：true if the primary selection has text, or false if it does not. |
| <a id="api-dd2981060dab2530"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasProperty(uint props, sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28473) | Return whether a property exists in a group of properties. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPropertyType<br>`props`：the properties to query.<br>`name`：the name of the property to query.<br>返回：true if the property exists, or false if it doesn't. |
| <a id="api-dee21f9e66073b88"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasRectIntersection(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* A, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* B)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30817) | Determine whether two rectangles intersect. If either pointer is NULL the function will return false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRectIntersection<br>`A`：an SDL_Rect structure representing the first rectangle.<br>`B`：an SDL_Rect structure representing the second rectangle.<br>返回：true if there is an intersection, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-70e308c93685be62"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasRectIntersectionFloat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* A, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* B)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48298) | Determine whether two rectangles intersect with float precision. If either pointer is NULL the function will return false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRectIntersectionFloat<br>`A`：an SDL_FRect structure representing the first rectangle.<br>`B`：an SDL_FRect structure representing the second rectangle.<br>返回：true if there is an intersection, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-8ce3b1063e0c8afb"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasScreenKeyboardSupport()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25515) | Check whether the platform has screen keyboard support. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_StartTextInput @sa SDL_ScreenKeyboardShown<br>返回：true if the platform has some screen keyboard support or false if not. |
| <a id="api-9f193fe790b11434"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasSse()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52892) | Determine whether the CPU has SSE features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasSSE2 @sa SDL_HasSSE3 @sa SDL_HasSSE41 @sa SDL_HasSSE42<br>返回：true if the CPU has SSE features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-d42a4f1f9148b65d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasSse2()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26313) | Determine whether the CPU has SSE2 features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasSSE @sa SDL_HasSSE3 @sa SDL_HasSSE41 @sa SDL_HasSSE42<br>返回：true if the CPU has SSE2 features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-ff0c136fc73c99ee"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasSse3()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21339) | Determine whether the CPU has SSE3 features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasSSE @sa SDL_HasSSE2 @sa SDL_HasSSE41 @sa SDL_HasSSE42<br>返回：true if the CPU has SSE3 features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-545fee1bab9618a5"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasSse41()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46544) | Determine whether the CPU has SSE4.1 features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasSSE @sa SDL_HasSSE2 @sa SDL_HasSSE3 @sa SDL_HasSSE42<br>返回：true if the CPU has SSE4.1 features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-f61a1c11aec64d84"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HasSse42()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38963) | Determine whether the CPU has SSE4.2 features.
