This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasSSE @sa SDL_HasSSE2 @sa SDL_HasSSE3 @sa SDL_HasSSE41<br>返回：true if the CPU has SSE4.2 features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-ae1b85165fd201b9"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidBleScan(byte active)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27178) | Start or stop a BLE scan on iOS and tvOS to pair Steam Controllers. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`active`：true to start the scan, false to stop the scan. |
| <a id="api-d1ce8f9f22436675"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidClose(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47068) | Close a HID device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-25494bebcfddd514"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidDeviceChangeCount()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49619) | Check to see if devices may have been added or removed. Enumerating the HID devices is an expensive operation, so you can call this to see if there have been any system device changes since the last call to this function. A change in the counter returned doesn't necessarily mean that anything has changed, but you can call SDL_hid_enumerate() to get an updated device list. Calling this function for the first time may cause a thread or other system resource to be allocated to track device change notifications. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_hid_enumerate<br>返回：a change counter that is incremented with each potential device change, or 0 if device change detection isn't available. |
| <a id="api-a5c2fcac981d974f"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDeviceInfo* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidEnumerate(ushort vendor_id, ushort product_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49574) | Enumerate the HID Devices. This function returns a linked list of all the HID devices attached to the system which match vendor_id and product_id. If `vendor_id` is set to 0 then any vendor matches. If `product_id` is set to 0 then any product matches. If `vendor_id` and `product_id` are both set to 0, then all HID devices will be returned. By default SDL will only enumerate controllers, to reduce risk of hanging or crashing on bad drivers, but SDL_HINT_HIDAPI_ENUMERATE_ONLY_CONTROLLERS can be set to "0" to enumerate all HID devices. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_hid_device_change_count<br>`vendor_id`：the Vendor ID (VID) of the types of device to open, or 0 to match any vendor.<br>`product_id`：the Product ID (PID) of the types of device to open, or 0 to match any product.<br>返回：a pointer to a linked list of type SDL_hid_device_info, containing information about the HID devices attached to the system, or NULL in the case of failure. Free this linked list by calling SDL_hid_free_enumeration(). |
| <a id="api-0a53e4e954082b78"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidExit()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50526) | Finalize the HIDAPI library. This function frees all of the static data associated with HIDAPI. It should be called at the end of execution to avoid memory leaks. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_hid_init<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d5e4e6e3ebcfa03e"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidFreeEnumeration(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDeviceInfo* devs)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36966) | Free an enumeration linked list. This function frees a linked list created by SDL_hid_enumerate(). @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`devs`：pointer to a list of struct_device returned from SDL_hid_enumerate(). |
| <a id="api-16912b76f0bdb1a2"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDeviceInfo* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidGetDeviceInfo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44722) | Get the device info from a HID device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>返回：a pointer to the SDL_hid_device_info for this hid_device or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This struct is valid until the device is closed with SDL_hid_close(). |
| <a id="api-78acdbabd021e86d"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidGetFeatureReport(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, byte* data, ulong length)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16754) | Get a feature report from a HID device. Set the first byte of `data` to the Report ID of the report to be read. Make sure to allow space for this extra byte in `data`. Upon return, the first byte will still contain the Report ID, and the report data will start in data[1]. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`data`：a buffer to put the read data into, including the Report ID. Set the first byte of `data` to the Report ID of the report to be read, or set it to zero if your device does not use numbered reports.<br>`length`：the number of bytes to read, including an extra byte for the report ID. The buffer can be longer than the actual report.<br>返回：the number of bytes read plus one for the report ID (which is still in the first byte), or -1 on on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-533f18f931ecab70"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidGetIndexedString(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, int string_index, ushort* @string, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29615) | Get a string from a HID device, based on its string index. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`string_index`：the index of the string to get.<br>`string`：a wide string buffer to put the data into.<br>`maxlen`：the length of the buffer in multiples of wchar_t.<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c42dce717d6bfb9f"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidGetInputReport(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, byte* data, ulong length)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21293) | Get an input report from a HID device. Set the first byte of `data` to the Report ID of the report to be read. Make sure to allow space for this extra byte in `data`. Upon return, the first byte will still contain the Report ID, and the report data will start in data[1]. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`data`：a buffer to put the read data into, including the Report ID. Set the first byte of `data` to the Report ID of the report to be read, or set it to zero if your device does not use numbered reports.<br>`length`：the number of bytes to read, including an extra byte for the report ID. The buffer can be longer than the actual report.<br>返回：the number of bytes read plus one for the report ID (which is still in the first byte), or -1 on on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-91927c5a8a032aa4"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidGetManufacturerString(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, ushort* @string, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30867) | Get The Manufacturer String from a HID device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`string`：a wide string buffer to put the data into.<br>`maxlen`：the length of the buffer in multiples of wchar_t.<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3d90170249f61ae0"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidGetProductString(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, ushort* @string, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27877) | Get The Product String from a HID device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`string`：a wide string buffer to put the data into.<br>`maxlen`：the length of the buffer in multiples of wchar_t.<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-426ad382e05e2869"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidGetProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43752) | Get the properties associated with an SDL_hid_device. The following read-only properties are provided by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_HIDAPI_LIBUSB_DEVICE_HANDLE_POINTER`: the libusb_device_handle associated with the device, if it was opened using libusb. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-2665492071a6eb9d"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidGetReportDescriptor(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, byte* buf, ulong buf_size)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47966) | Get a report descriptor from a HID device. User has to provide a preallocated buffer where descriptor will be copied to. The recommended size for a preallocated buffer is 4096 bytes. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`buf`：the buffer to copy descriptor into.<br>`buf_size`：the size of the buffer in bytes.<br>返回：the number of bytes actually copied or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-59d087c9688a25a0"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidGetSerialNumberString(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, ushort* @string, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43800) | Get The Serial Number String from a HID device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`string`：a wide string buffer to put the data into.<br>`maxlen`：the length of the buffer in multiples of wchar_t.<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5e58113cfc5dfe8d"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidInit()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52262) | Initialize the HIDAPI library. This function initializes the HIDAPI library. Calling it is not strictly necessary, as it will be called automatically by SDL_hid_enumerate(), SDL_hid_open(), and SDL_hid_open_path() if needed. This function should be called at the beginning of execution however, if there is a chance of HIDAPI handles being opened by different threads simultaneously. Each call to this function should have a matching call to SDL_hid_exit() @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_hid_exit<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8c389905fc1c5ee7"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidOpen(ushort vendor_id, ushort product_id, ushort* serial_number)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23888) | Open a HID device using a Vendor ID (VID), Product ID (PID) and optionally a serial number. If `serial_number` is NULL, the first device with the specified VID and PID is opened. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`vendor_id`：the Vendor ID (VID) of the device to open.<br>`product_id`：the Product ID (PID) of the device to open.<br>`serial_number`：the Serial Number of the device to open (Optionally NULL).<br>返回：a pointer to a SDL_hid_device object on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d2f1ff74ee02294b"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidOpenPath(sbyte* path)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32960) | Open a HID device by its path name. The path name be determined by calling SDL_hid_enumerate(), or a platform-specific path name can be used (eg: /dev/hidraw0 on Linux). @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`path`：the path name of the device to open.<br>返回：a pointer to a SDL_hid_device object on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-411cb666a08454c2"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidRead(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, byte* data, ulong length)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43597) | Read an Input report from a HID device. Input reports are returned to the host through the INTERRUPT IN endpoint. The first byte will contain the Report number if the device uses numbered reports. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`data`：a buffer to put the read data into.<br>`length`：the number of bytes to read. For devices with multiple reports, make sure to read an extra byte for the report number.<br>返回：the actual number of bytes read and -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. If no packet was available to be read and the handle is in non-blocking mode, this function returns 0. |
| <a id="api-f4ace91a0a429032"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidReadTimeout(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, byte* data, ulong length, int milliseconds)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20343) | Read an Input report from a HID device with timeout. Input reports are returned to the host through the INTERRUPT IN endpoint. The first byte will contain the Report number if the device uses numbered reports. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`data`：a buffer to put the read data into.<br>`length`：the number of bytes to read. For devices with multiple reports, make sure to read an extra byte for the report number.<br>`milliseconds`：timeout in milliseconds or -1 for blocking wait.<br>返回：the actual number of bytes read and -1 on on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. If no packet was available to be read within the timeout period, this function returns 0. |
| <a id="api-dc5469130f7c4a39"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidSendFeatureReport(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, byte* data, ulong length)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16790) | Send a Feature report to the device. Feature reports are sent over the Control endpoint as a Set_Report transfer. The first byte of `data` must contain the Report ID. For devices which only support a single report, this must be set to 0x0. The remaining bytes contain the report data. Since the Report ID is mandatory, calls to SDL_hid_send_feature_report() will always contain one more byte than the report contains. For example, if a hid report is 16 bytes long, 17 bytes must be passed to SDL_hid_send_feature_report(): the Report ID (or 0x0, for devices which do not use numbered reports), followed by the report data (16 bytes). In this example, the length passed in would be 17. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`data`：the data to send, including the report number as the first byte.<br>`length`：the length in bytes of the data to send, including the report number.<br>返回：the actual number of bytes written and -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-021b9dff5265dcba"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidSetNonblocking(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, int nonblock)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51498) | Set the device handle to be non-blocking. In non-blocking mode calls to SDL_hid_read() will return immediately with a value of 0 if there is no data to be read. In blocking mode, SDL_hid_read() will wait (block) until there is data to read before returning. Nonblocking can be turned on and off at any time. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`nonblock`：enable or not the nonblocking reads - 1 to enable nonblocking - 0 to disable nonblocking.<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-2e7bd3503a57e8df"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HidWrite(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHidDevice* dev, byte* data, ulong length)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32652) | Write an Output report to a HID device. The first byte of `data` must contain the Report ID. For devices which only support a single report, this must be set to 0x0. The remaining bytes contain the report data. Since the Report ID is mandatory, calls to SDL_hid_write() will always contain one more byte than the report contains. For example, if a hid report is 16 bytes long, 17 bytes must be passed to SDL_hid_write(), the Report ID (or 0x0, for devices with a single report), followed by the report data (16 bytes). In this example, the length passed in would be 17. SDL_hid_write() will send the data on the first OUT endpoint, if one exists. If it does not, it will send the data through the Control Endpoint (Endpoint 0). @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dev`：a device handle returned from SDL_hid_open().<br>`data`：the data to send, including the report number as the first byte.<br>`length`：the length in bytes of the data to send.<br>返回：the actual number of bytes written and -1 on on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-86e0225f13c1743e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HideCursor()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34063) | Hide the cursor. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CursorVisible @sa SDL_ShowCursor<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e6aef086f45d9e9e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.HideWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26137) | Hide a window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ShowWindow @sa SDL_WINDOW_HIDDEN<br>`window`：the window to hide.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-293ae7a8886bd447"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IOvprintf(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* context, sbyte* fmt, sbyte* ap)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19952) | Print to an SDL_IOStream data stream. This function does formatted printing to the stream. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_IOprintf @sa SDL_WriteIO<br>`context`：a pointer to an SDL_IOStream structure.<br>`fmt`：a printf() style format string.<br>`ap`：a variable argument list.<br>返回：the number of bytes written or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5cd8b0c80bfc6c5e"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Iconv(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIconvDataT* cd, sbyte** inbuf, ulong* inbytesleft, sbyte** outbuf, ulong* outbytesleft)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45097) | This function converts text between encodings, reading from and writing to a buffer. It returns the number of successful conversions on success. On error, SDL_ICONV_E2BIG is returned when the output buffer is too small, or SDL_ICONV_EILSEQ is returned when an invalid input sequence is encountered, or SDL_ICONV_EINVAL is returned when an incomplete input sequence is encountered. On exit: - inbuf will point to the beginning of the next multibyte sequence. On error, this is the location of the problematic input sequence. On success, this is the end of the input sequence. - inbytesleft will be set to the number of bytes left to convert, which will be 0 on success. - outbuf will point to the location where to store the next output byte. - outbytesleft will be set to the number of bytes left in the output buffer. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_iconv_t from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_iconv_open @sa SDL_iconv_close @sa SDL_iconv_string<br>`cd`：The character set conversion context, created in SDL_iconv_open().<br>`inbuf`：Address of variable that points to the first character of the input sequence.<br>`inbytesleft`：The number of bytes in the input buffer.<br>`outbuf`：Address of variable that points to the output buffer.<br>`outbytesleft`：The number of bytes in the output buffer.<br>返回：the number of conversions on success, or a negative error code. |
| <a id="api-ed37362fff78a8b0"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IconvClose(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIconvDataT* cd)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29979) | This function frees a context used for character set conversion. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_iconv @sa SDL_iconv_open @sa SDL_iconv_string<br>`cd`：The character set conversion handle.<br>返回：0 on success, or -1 on failure. |
| <a id="api-307ed116be2152c1"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIconvDataT* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IconvOpen(sbyte* tocode, sbyte* fromcode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40913) | This function allocates a context for the specified character set conversion. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_iconv @sa SDL_iconv_close @sa SDL_iconv_string<br>`tocode`：The target character encoding, must not be NULL.<br>`fromcode`：The source character encoding, must not be NULL.<br>返回：a handle that must be freed with SDL_iconv_close, or SDL_ICONV_ERROR on failure. |
| <a id="api-35819119b23c4ab3"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IconvString(sbyte* tocode, sbyte* fromcode, sbyte* inbuf, ulong inbytesleft)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24889) | Helper function to convert a string's encoding in one call. This function converts a buffer or string between encodings in one pass. The string does not need to be NULL-terminated; this function operates on the number of bytes specified in `inbytesleft` whether there is a NULL character anywhere in the buffer. The returned string is owned by the caller, and should be passed to SDL_free when no longer needed. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_iconv_open @sa SDL_iconv_close @sa SDL_iconv<br>`tocode`：the character encoding of the output string. Examples are "UTF-8", "UCS-4", etc.<br>`fromcode`：the character encoding of data in `inbuf`.<br>`inbuf`：the string to convert to a different encoding.<br>`inbytesleft`：the size of the input string _in bytes_.<br>返回：a new string, converted to the new encoding, or NULL on error. |
| <a id="api-159d995a2de66beb"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Init(uint flags)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43414) | Initialize the SDL library. SDL_Init() simply forwards to calling SDL_InitSubSystem(). Therefore, the two may be used interchangeably. Though for readability of your code SDL_InitSubSystem() might be preferred. The file I/O (for example: SDL_IOFromFile) and threading (SDL_CreateThread) subsystems are initialized by default. Message boxes (SDL_ShowSimpleMessageBox) also attempt to work without initializing the video subsystem, in hopes of being useful in showing an error dialog when SDL_Init fails. You must specifically initialize other subsystems if you use them in your application. Logging (such as SDL_Log) works without initialization, too. `flags` may be any of the following OR'd together: - `SDL_INIT_AUDIO`: audio subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_VIDEO`: video subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem, should be initialized on the main thread. - `SDL_INIT_JOYSTICK`: joystick subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_HAPTIC`: haptic (force feedback) subsystem - `SDL_INIT_GAMEPAD`: gamepad subsystem; automatically initializes the joystick subsystem - `SDL_INIT_EVENTS`: events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_SENSOR`: sensor subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_CAMERA`: camera subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem Subsystem initialization is ref-counted, you must call SDL_QuitSubSystem() for each SDL_InitSubSystem() to correctly shutdown a subsystem manually (or call SDL_Quit() to force shutdown). If a subsystem is already loaded then this call will increase the ref-count and return. Consider reporting some basic metadata about your application before calling SDL_Init, using either SDL_SetAppMetadata() or SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAppMetadata @sa SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty @sa SDL_InitSubSystem @sa SDL_Quit @sa SDL_SetMainReady @sa SDL_WasInit<br>`flags`：subsystem initialization flags.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a49eb0d3d9047d43"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.InitHapticRumble(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50853) | Initialize a haptic device for simple rumble playback. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PlayHapticRumble @sa SDL_StopHapticRumble @sa SDL_HapticRumbleSupported<br>`haptic`：the haptic device to initialize for simple rumble playback.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8be5401f9ff906e6"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.InitSubSystem(uint flags)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44122) | Compatibility function to initialize the SDL library. This function and SDL_Init() are interchangeable. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_Init @sa SDL_Quit @sa SDL_QuitSubSystem<br>`flags`：any of the flags used by SDL_Init(); see SDL_Init for details.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-84de542ed81122ed"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.InsertGpuDebugLabel(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCommandBuffer* command_buffer, sbyte* text)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36592) | Inserts an arbitrary string label into the command buffer callstream. Useful for debugging. On Direct3D 12, using SDL_InsertGPUDebugLabel requires WinPixEventRuntime.dll to be in your PATH or in the same directory as your executable. See [here](https://devblogs.microsoft.com/pix/winpixeventruntime/) for instructions on how to obtain it. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`command_buffer`：a command buffer.<br>`text`：a UTF-8 string constant to insert as the label. |
| <a id="api-7a146fe1754bd3ae"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.InsertTrayEntryAt(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayMenu* menu, int pos, sbyte* label, uint flags)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32222) | Insert a tray entry at a given position. If label is NULL, the entry will be a separator. Many functions won't work for an entry that is a separator. An entry does not need to be destroyed; it will be destroyed with the tray. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_TrayEntryFlags @sa SDL_GetTrayEntries @sa SDL_RemoveTrayEntry @sa SDL_GetTrayEntryParent<br>`menu`：the menu to append the entry to.<br>`pos`：the desired position for the new entry. Entries at or following this place will be moved. If pos is -1, the entry is appended.<br>`label`：the text to be displayed on the entry, in UTF-8 encoding, or NULL for a separator.<br>`flags`：a combination of flags, some of which are mandatory.<br>返回：the newly created entry, or NULL if pos is out of bounds. |
| <a id="api-7df0fed56a88c7fc"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IoFromConstMem(void* mem, ulong size)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35855) | Use this function to prepare a read-only memory buffer for use with SDL_IOStream. This function sets up an SDL_IOStream struct based on a memory area of a certain size. It assumes the memory area is not writable. Attempting to write to this SDL_IOStream stream will report an error without writing to the memory buffer. This memory buffer is not copied by the SDL_IOStream; the pointer you provide must remain valid until you close the stream. If you need to write to a memory buffer, you should use SDL_IOFromMem() with a writable buffer of memory instead. The following properties will be set at creation time by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_MEMORY_POINTER`: this will be the `mem` parameter that was passed to this function. - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_MEMORY_SIZE_NUMBER`: this will be the `size` parameter that was passed to this function. Additionally, the following properties are recognized: - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_MEMORY_FREE_FUNC_POINTER`: if this property is set to a non-NULL value it will be interpreted as a function of SDL_free_func type and called with the passed `mem` pointer when closing the stream. By default it is unset, i.e., the memory will not be freed. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_IOFromMem @sa SDL_CloseIO @sa SDL_ReadIO @sa SDL_SeekIO @sa SDL_TellIO<br>`mem`：a pointer to a read-only buffer to feed an SDL_IOStream stream.<br>`size`：the buffer size, in bytes.<br>返回：a pointer to a new SDL_IOStream structure or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a81c5df73d4b9711"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IoFromDynamicMem()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39771) | Use this function to create an SDL_IOStream that is backed by dynamically allocated memory. This supports the following properties to provide access to the memory and control over allocations: - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_DYNAMIC_MEMORY_POINTER`: a pointer to the internal memory of the stream. This can be set to NULL to transfer ownership of the memory to the application, which should free the memory with SDL_free(). If this is done, the next operation on the stream must be SDL_CloseIO(). - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_DYNAMIC_CHUNKSIZE_NUMBER`: memory will be allocated in multiples of this size, defaulting to 1024. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CloseIO @sa SDL_ReadIO @sa SDL_SeekIO @sa SDL_TellIO @sa SDL_WriteIO<br>返回：a pointer to a new SDL_IOStream structure or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-2dc516790616161e"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IoFromFile(sbyte* file, sbyte* mode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30256) | Use this function to create a new SDL_IOStream structure for reading from and/or writing to a named file. The `mode` string is treated roughly the same as in a call to the C library's fopen(), even if SDL doesn't happen to use fopen() behind the scenes. Available `mode` strings: - "r": Open a file for reading. The file must exist. - "w": Create an empty file for writing. If a file with the same name already exists its content is erased and the file is treated as a new empty file. - "wx": Create an empty file for writing. If a file with the same name already exists, the call fails. - "a": Append to a file. Writing operations append data at the end of the file. The file is created if it does not exist. - "r+": Open a file for update both reading and writing. The file must exist. - "w+": Create an empty file for both reading and writing. If a file with the same name already exists its content is erased and the file is treated as a new empty file. - "w+x": Create an empty file for both reading and writing. If a file with the same name already exists, the call fails. - "a+": Open a file for reading and appending. All writing operations are performed at the end of the file, protecting the previous content to be overwritten. You can reposition (fseek, rewind) the internal pointer to anywhere in the file for reading, but writing operations will move it back to the end of file. The file is created if it does not exist. **NOTE**: In order to open a file as a binary file, a "b" character has to be included in the `mode` string. This additional "b" character can either be appended at the end of the string (thus making the following compound modes: "rb", "wb", "ab", "r+b", "w+b", "a+b") or be inserted between the letter and the "+" sign for the mixed modes ("rb+", "wb+", "ab+"). Additional characters may follow the sequence, although they should have no effect. For example, "t" is sometimes appended to make explicit the file is a text file. This function supports Unicode filenames, but they must be encoded in UTF-8 format, regardless of the underlying operating system. In Android, SDL_IOFromFile() can be used to open content:// URIs. As a fallback, SDL_IOFromFile() will transparently open a matching filename in the app's `assets`. Closing the SDL_IOStream will close SDL's internal file handle. The following properties may be set at creation time by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_WINDOWS_HANDLE_POINTER`: a pointer, that can be cast to a win32 `HANDLE`, that this SDL_IOStream is using to access the filesystem. If the program isn't running on Windows, or SDL used some other method to access the filesystem, this property will not be set. - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_STDIO_FILE_POINTER`: a pointer, that can be cast to a stdio `FILE *`, that this SDL_IOStream is using to access the filesystem. If SDL used some other method to access the filesystem, this property will not be set. PLEASE NOTE that if SDL is using a different C runtime than your app, trying to use this pointer will almost certainly result in a crash! This is mostly a problem on Windows; make sure you build SDL and your app with the same compiler and settings to avoid it. - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_FILE_DESCRIPTOR_NUMBER`: a file descriptor that this SDL_IOStream is using to access the filesystem. - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_ANDROID_AASSET_POINTER`: a pointer, that can be cast to an Android NDK `AAsset *`, that this SDL_IOStream is using to access the filesystem. If SDL used some other method to access the filesystem, this property will not be set. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CloseIO @sa SDL_FlushIO @sa SDL_ReadIO @sa SDL_SeekIO @sa SDL_TellIO @sa SDL_WriteIO<br>`file`：a UTF-8 string representing the filename to open.<br>`mode`：an ASCII string representing the mode to be used for opening the file.<br>返回：a pointer to the SDL_IOStream structure that is created or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f866a255b380fd37"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IoFromMem(void* mem, ulong size)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27041) | Use this function to prepare a read-write memory buffer for use with SDL_IOStream. This function sets up an SDL_IOStream struct based on a memory area of a certain size, for both read and write access. This memory buffer is not copied by the SDL_IOStream; the pointer you provide must remain valid until you close the stream. If you need to make sure the SDL_IOStream never writes to the memory buffer, you should use SDL_IOFromConstMem() with a read-only buffer of memory instead. The following properties will be set at creation time by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_MEMORY_POINTER`: this will be the `mem` parameter that was passed to this function. - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_MEMORY_SIZE_NUMBER`: this will be the `size` parameter that was passed to this function. Additionally, the following properties are recognized: - `SDL_PROP_IOSTREAM_MEMORY_FREE_FUNC_POINTER`: if this property is set to a non-NULL value it will be interpreted as a function of SDL_free_func type and called with the passed `mem` pointer when closing the stream. By default it is unset, i.e., the memory will not be freed. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_IOFromConstMem @sa SDL_CloseIO @sa SDL_FlushIO @sa SDL_ReadIO @sa SDL_SeekIO @sa SDL_TellIO @sa SDL_WriteIO<br>`mem`：a pointer to a buffer to feed an SDL_IOStream stream.<br>`size`：the buffer size, in bytes.<br>返回：a pointer to a new SDL_IOStream structure or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9944b9325efaf11a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IsAudioDevicePhysical(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36221) | Determine if an audio device is physical (instead of logical). An SDL_AudioDeviceID that represents physical hardware is a physical device; there is one for each piece of hardware that SDL can see. Logical devices are created by calling SDL_OpenAudioDevice or SDL_OpenAudioDeviceStream, and while each is associated with a physical device, there can be any number of logical devices on one physical device. For the most part, logical and physical IDs are interchangeable--if you try to open a logical device, SDL understands to assign that effort to the underlying physical device, etc. However, it might be useful to know if an arbitrary device ID is physical or logical. This function reports which. This function may return either true or false for invalid device IDs. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`devid`：the device ID to query.<br>返回：true if devid is a physical device, false if it is logical. |
| <a id="api-630c39584b1c547a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IsAudioDevicePlayback(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22681) | Determine if an audio device is a playback device (instead of recording). This function may return either true or false for invalid device IDs. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`devid`：the device ID to query.<br>返回：true if devid is a playback device, false if it is recording. |
| <a id="api-d4da19e97049b442"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IsGamepad(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38178) | Check if the given joystick is supported by the gamepad interface. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoysticks @sa SDL_OpenGamepad<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：true if the given joystick is supported by the gamepad interface, false if it isn't or it's an invalid index. |
| <a id="api-374e59ecaa1fa6dd"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IsJoystickHaptic(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30058) | Query if a joystick has haptic features. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenHapticFromJoystick<br>`joystick`：the SDL_Joystick to test for haptic capabilities.<br>返回：true if the joystick is haptic or false if it isn't. |
| <a id="api-bcbc31aab625fc1d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IsJoystickVirtual(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49996) | Query whether or not a joystick is virtual. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：true if the joystick is virtual, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-792e0e99594e5c1c"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IsMainThread()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19106) | Return whether this is the main thread. On Apple platforms, the main thread is the thread that runs your program's main() entry point. On other platforms, the main thread is the one that calls SDL_Init(SDL_INIT_VIDEO), which should usually be the one that runs your program's main() entry point. If you are using the main callbacks, SDL_AppInit(), SDL_AppIterate(), and SDL_AppQuit() are all called on the main thread. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RunOnMainThread<br>返回：true if this thread is the main thread, or false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-44acf00ce6bbe96f"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IsMouseHaptic()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23001) | Query whether or not the current mouse has haptic capabilities. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenHapticFromMouse<br>返回：true if the mouse is haptic or false if it isn't. |
| <a id="api-be0a812fbe9745c0"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IsTablet()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21931) | Query if the current device is a tablet. If SDL can't determine this, it will return false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true if the device is a tablet, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-1d8d1a8d96695fb5"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.IsTv()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17557) | Query if the current device is a TV. If SDL can't determine this, it will return false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true if the device is a TV, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-e8524688a9d29fd9"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isalnum(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43524) | Query if a character is alphabetic (a letter) or a number. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values for English 'a-z', 'A-Z', and '0-9' as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-7a03ab606ff4ba15"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isalpha(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51434) | Query if a character is alphabetic (a letter). **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values for English 'a-z' and 'A-Z' as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-f14dc1911ef7fc94"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isblank(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51902) | Report if a character is blank (a space or tab). **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values 0x20 (space) or 0x9 (tab) as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-811a77814cbfabb5"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Iscntrl(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52134) | Report if a character is a control character. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values 0 through 0x1F, and 0x7F, as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-df9c027b9ca8b311"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isdigit(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22816) | Report if a character is a numeric digit. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values '0' (0x30) through '9' (0x39), as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-3c43ba558ee20f64"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isgraph(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35645) | Report if a character is any "printable" except space. Be advised that "printable" has a definition that goes back to text terminals from the dawn of computing, making this a sort of special case function that is not suitable for Unicode (or most any) text management. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this is equivalent to `(SDL_isprint(x)) & & ((x) != ' ')`. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_isprint<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-2e67e83d4c748134"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isinf(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42512) | Return whether the value is infinity. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_isinff<br>`x`：double-precision floating point value.<br>返回：non-zero if the value is infinity, 0 otherwise. |
| <a id="api-87c6537f8b3fa91e"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isinff(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18010) | Return whether the value is infinity. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_isinf<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：non-zero if the value is infinity, 0 otherwise. |
| <a id="api-807a964110eae502"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Islower(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37539) | Report if a character is lower case. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values 'a' through 'z' as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-9e4b7128a6131a0f"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isnan(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45797) | Return whether the value is NaN. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_isnanf<br>`x`：double-precision floating point value.<br>返回：non-zero if the value is NaN, 0 otherwise. |
| <a id="api-5315a7657cbd530c"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isnanf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33052) | Return whether the value is NaN. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_isnan<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：non-zero if the value is NaN, 0 otherwise. |
| <a id="api-1b2918c109f17214"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isprint(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19394) | Report if a character is "printable". Be advised that "printable" has a definition that goes back to text terminals from the dawn of computing, making this a sort of special case function that is not suitable for Unicode (or most any) text management. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values ' ' (0x20) through '~' (0x7E) as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-5e339d084b227cab"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Ispunct(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40360) | Report if a character is a punctuation mark. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this is equivalent to `((SDL_isgraph(x)) & & (!SDL_isalnum(x)))`. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_isgraph @sa SDL_isalnum<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-238805babe7270bc"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isspace(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42850) | Report if a character is whitespace. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat the following ASCII values as true: - space (0x20) - tab (0x09) - newline (0x0A) - vertical tab (0x0B) - form feed (0x0C) - return (0x0D) @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-470b5fd39791d649"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isupper(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39102) | Report if a character is upper case. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values 'A' through 'Z' as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-dad528bb2d6cb7f2"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Isxdigit(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45731) | Report if a character is a hexadecimal digit. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values 'A' through 'F', 'a' through 'f', and '0' through '9', as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-dfae2c8caacdda41"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Itoa(int value, sbyte* str, int radix)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24250) | Convert an integer into a string. This requires a radix to specified for string format. Specifying 10 produces a decimal number, 16 hexadecimal, etc. Must be in the range of 2 to 36. Note that this function will overflow a buffer if `str` is not large enough to hold the output! It may be safer to use SDL_snprintf to clamp output, or SDL_asprintf to allocate a buffer. Otherwise, it doesn't hurt to allocate much more space than you expect to use (and don't forget possible negative signs, null terminator bytes, etc). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_uitoa @sa SDL_ltoa @sa SDL_lltoa<br>`value`：the integer to convert.<br>`str`：the buffer to write the string into.<br>`radix`：the radix to use for string generation.<br>返回：`str`. |
| <a id="api-d64f8eab11fbb6ff"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.JoystickConnected(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22445) | Get the status of a specified joystick. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`joystick`：the joystick to query.<br>返回：true if the joystick has been opened, false if it has not; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-fbd9e53586a5f333"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.JoystickEventsEnabled()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36774) | Query the state of joystick event processing. If joystick events are disabled, you must call SDL_UpdateJoysticks() yourself and check the state of the joystick when you want joystick information. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetJoystickEventsEnabled<br>返回：true if joystick events are being processed, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-cf032cfde7b633fb"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.KillProcess(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLProcess* process, byte force)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29424) | Stop a process. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateProcess @sa SDL_CreateProcessWithProperties @sa SDL_WaitProcess @sa SDL_DestroyProcess<br>`process`：The process to stop.<br>`force`：true to terminate the process immediately, false to try to stop the process gracefully. In general you should try to stop the process gracefully first as terminating a process may leave it with half-written data or in some other unstable state.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c03f7e51cb0b3873"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Lltoa(long value, sbyte* str, int radix)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18791) | Convert a long long integer into a string. This requires a radix to specified for string format. Specifying 10 produces a decimal number, 16 hexadecimal, etc. Must be in the range of 2 to 36. Note that this function will overflow a buffer if `str` is not large enough to hold the output! It may be safer to use SDL_snprintf to clamp output, or SDL_asprintf to allocate a buffer. Otherwise, it doesn't hurt to allocate much more space than you expect to use (and don't forget possible negative signs, null terminator bytes, etc). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ulltoa @sa SDL_itoa @sa SDL_ltoa<br>`value`：the long long integer to convert.<br>`str`：the buffer to write the string into.<br>`radix`：the radix to use for string generation.<br>返回：`str`. |
| <a id="api-92ac82ec81624237"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadBmp(sbyte* file)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39239) | Load a BMP image from a file. The new surface should be freed with SDL_DestroySurface(). Not doing so will result in a memory leak. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_DestroySurface @sa SDL_LoadBMP_IO @sa SDL_SaveBMP<br>`file`：the BMP file to load.<br>返回：a pointer to a new SDL_Surface structure or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b89bf08e563f575b"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadBmpIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* src, byte closeio)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48857) | Load a BMP image from a seekable SDL data stream. The new surface should be freed with SDL_DestroySurface(). Not doing so will result in a memory leak. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_DestroySurface @sa SDL_LoadBMP @sa SDL_SaveBMP_IO<br>`src`：the data stream for the surface.<br>`closeio`：if true, calls SDL_CloseIO() on `src` before returning, even in the case of an error.<br>返回：a pointer to a new SDL_Surface structure or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a8556d1bc3943674"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadFile(sbyte* file, ulong* datasize)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38519) | Load all the data from a file path. The data is allocated with a zero byte at the end (null terminated) for convenience. This extra byte is not included in the value reported via `datasize`. The data should be freed with SDL_free(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LoadFile_IO @sa SDL_SaveFile<br>`file`：the path to read all available data from.<br>`datasize`：if not NULL, will store the number of bytes read.<br>返回：the data or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a93454c9fa4a4746"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadFileAsync(sbyte* file, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAsyncIOQueue* queue, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20610) | Load all the data from a file path, asynchronously. This function returns as quickly as possible; it does not wait for the read to complete. On a successful return, this work will continue in the background. If the work begins, even failure is asynchronous: a failing return value from this function only means the work couldn't start at all. The data is allocated with a zero byte at the end (null terminated) for convenience. This extra byte is not included in SDL_AsyncIOOutcome's bytes_transferred value. This function will allocate the buffer to contain the file. It must be deallocated by calling SDL_free() on SDL_AsyncIOOutcome's buffer field after completion. An SDL_AsyncIOQueue must be specified. The newly-created task will be added to it when it completes its work. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LoadFile_IO<br>`file`：the path to read all available data from.<br>`queue`：a queue to add the new SDL_AsyncIO to.<br>`userdata`：an app-defined pointer that will be provided with the task results.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-59f07ef27d5be7d9"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadFileIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* src, ulong* datasize, byte closeio)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27799) | Load all the data from an SDL data stream. The data is allocated with a zero byte at the end (null terminated) for convenience. This extra byte is not included in the value reported via `datasize`. The data should be freed with SDL_free(). @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LoadFile @sa SDL_SaveFile_IO<br>`src`：the SDL_IOStream to read all available data from.<br>`datasize`：a pointer filled in with the number of bytes read, may be NULL.<br>`closeio`：if true, calls SDL_CloseIO() on `src` before returning, even in the case of an error.<br>返回：the data or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c04c3ebf4088dd29"></a>[`public delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void> Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadFunction(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSharedObject* handle, sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24768) | Look up the address of the named function in a shared object. This function pointer is no longer valid after calling SDL_UnloadObject(). This function can only look up C function names. Other languages may have name mangling and intrinsic language support that varies from compiler to compiler. Make sure you declare your function pointers with the same calling convention as the actual library function. Your code will crash mysteriously if you do not do this. If the requested function doesn't exist, NULL is returned. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LoadObject<br>`handle`：a valid shared object handle returned by SDL_LoadObject().<br>`name`：the name of the function to look up.<br>返回：a pointer to the function or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7c56888c8fa1f8cf"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSharedObject* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadObject(sbyte* sofile)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24960) | Dynamically load a shared object. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LoadFunction @sa SDL_UnloadObject<br>`sofile`：a system-dependent name of the object file.<br>返回：an opaque pointer to the object handle or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ecea8077c264cd41"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadPng(sbyte* file)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28501) | Load a PNG image from a file. This is intended as a convenience function for loading images from trusted sources. If you want to load arbitrary images you should use libpng or another image loading library designed with security in mind. The new surface should be freed with SDL_DestroySurface(). Not doing so will result in a memory leak. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_DestroySurface @sa SDL_LoadPNG_IO @sa SDL_SavePNG<br>`file`：the PNG file to load.<br>返回：a pointer to a new SDL_Surface structure or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f1c8ed20958ad3fb"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadPngIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* src, byte closeio)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33922) | Load a PNG image from a seekable SDL data stream. This is intended as a convenience function for loading images from trusted sources. If you want to load arbitrary images you should use libpng or another image loading library designed with security in mind. The new surface should be freed with SDL_DestroySurface(). Not doing so will result in a memory leak. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_DestroySurface @sa SDL_LoadPNG @sa SDL_SavePNG_IO<br>`src`：the data stream for the surface.<br>`closeio`：if true, calls SDL_CloseIO() on `src` before returning, even in the case of an error.<br>返回：a pointer to a new SDL_Surface structure or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c5e0d9c98c919cce"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadSurface(sbyte* file)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52729) | Load a BMP or PNG image from a file. The new surface should be freed with SDL_DestroySurface(). Not doing so will result in a memory leak. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_DestroySurface @sa SDL_LoadSurface_IO<br>`file`：the file to load.<br>返回：a pointer to a new SDL_Surface structure or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-76abdf72810646a9"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadSurfaceIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* src, byte closeio)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20706) | Load a BMP or PNG image from a seekable SDL data stream. The new surface should be freed with SDL_DestroySurface(). Not doing so will result in a memory leak. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_DestroySurface @sa SDL_LoadSurface<br>`src`：the data stream for the surface.<br>`closeio`：if true, calls SDL_CloseIO() on `src` before returning, even in the case of an error.<br>返回：a pointer to a new SDL_Surface structure or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a30f7bfb820efac8"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadWav(sbyte* path, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioSpec* spec, byte** audio_buf, uint* audio_len)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42269) | Loads a WAV from a file path. This is a convenience function that is effectively the same as: ```c SDL_LoadWAV_IO(SDL_IOFromFile(path, "rb"), true, spec, audio_buf, audio_len); ``` This function returns false if the .WAV file cannot be opened, uses an unknown data format, or is corrupt; call SDL_GetError() for more information. When the application is done with the data returned in `audio_buf`, it should call SDL_free() to dispose of it. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_free @sa SDL_LoadWAV_IO<br>`path`：the file path of the WAV file to open.<br>`spec`：a pointer to an SDL_AudioSpec that will be set to the WAVE data's format details on successful return.<br>`audio_buf`：a pointer filled with the audio data, allocated by the function.<br>`audio_len`：a pointer filled with the length of the audio data buffer in bytes.<br>返回：true on success. `audio_buf` will be filled with a pointer to an allocated buffer containing the audio data, and `audio_len` is filled with the length of that audio buffer in bytes. |
| <a id="api-0fbe1efeedaeb710"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LoadWavIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* src, byte closeio, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioSpec* spec, byte** audio_buf, uint* audio_len)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50507) | Load the audio data of a WAVE file into memory. Loading a WAVE file requires `src`, `spec`, `audio_buf` and `audio_len` to be valid pointers. The entire data portion of the file is then loaded into memory and decoded if necessary. Supported formats are RIFF WAVE files with the formats PCM (8, 16, 24, and 32 bits), IEEE Float (32 bits), Microsoft ADPCM and IMA ADPCM (4 bits), and A-law and mu-law (8 bits). Other formats are currently unsupported and cause an error. If this function succeeds, the return value is zero and the pointer to the audio data allocated by the function is written to `audio_buf` and its length in bytes to `audio_len`. The SDL_AudioSpec members `freq`, `channels`, and `format` are set to the values of the audio data in the buffer. It's necessary to use SDL_free() to free the audio data returned in `audio_buf` when it is no longer used. Because of the underspecification of the .WAV format, there are many problematic files in the wild that cause issues with strict decoders. To provide compatibility with these files, this decoder is lenient in regards to the truncation of the file, the fact chunk, and the size of the RIFF chunk. The hints `SDL_HINT_WAVE_RIFF_CHUNK_SIZE`, `SDL_HINT_WAVE_TRUNCATION`, and `SDL_HINT_WAVE_FACT_CHUNK` can be used to tune the behavior of the loading process. Any file that is invalid (due to truncation, corruption, or wrong values in the headers), too big, or unsupported causes an error. Additionally, any critical I/O error from the data source will terminate the loading process with an error. The function returns NULL on error and in all cases (with the exception of `src` being NULL), an appropriate error message will be set. It is required that the data source supports seeking. Example: ```c SDL_LoadWAV_IO(SDL_IOFromFile("sample.wav", "rb"), true, &spec , &buf , &len ); ``` Note that the SDL_LoadWAV function does this same thing for you, but in a less messy way: ```c SDL_LoadWAV("sample.wav", &spec , &buf , &len ); ``` This function returns false if the .WAV file cannot be opened, uses an unknown data format, or is corrupt; call SDL_GetError() for more information. When the application is done with the data returned in `audio_buf`, it should call SDL_free() to dispose of it. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_free @sa SDL_LoadWAV<br>`src`：the data source for the WAVE data.<br>`closeio`：if true, calls SDL_CloseIO() on `src` before returning, even in the case of an error.<br>`spec`：a pointer to an SDL_AudioSpec that will be set to the WAVE data's format details on successful return.<br>`audio_buf`：a pointer filled with the audio data, allocated by the function.<br>`audio_len`：a pointer filled with the length of the audio data buffer in bytes.<br>返回：true on success. `audio_buf` will be filled with a pointer to an allocated buffer containing the audio data, and `audio_len` is filled with the length of that audio buffer in bytes. |
| <a id="api-386a0196d9046f13"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LockAudioStream(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52563) | Lock an audio stream for serialized access. Each SDL_AudioStream has an internal mutex it uses to protect its data structures from threading conflicts. This function allows an app to lock that mutex, which could be useful if registering callbacks on this stream. One does not need to lock a stream to use in it most cases, as the stream manages this lock internally. However, this lock is held during callbacks, which may run from arbitrary threads at any time, so if an app needs to protect shared data during those callbacks, locking the stream guarantees that the callback is not running while the lock is held. As this is just a wrapper over SDL_LockMutex for an internal lock; it has all the same attributes (recursive locks are allowed, etc). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_UnlockAudioStream<br>`stream`：the audio stream to lock.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-fc24b3c0da7bf027"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LockJoysticks()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19844) | Locking for atomic access to the joystick API. The SDL joystick functions are thread-safe, however you can lock the joysticks while processing to guarantee that the joystick list won't change and joystick and gamepad events will not be delivered. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. |
| <a id="api-3b9fb646312cf1ad"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LockMutex(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLMutex* mutex)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39922) | Lock the mutex. This will block until the mutex is available, which is to say it is in the unlocked state and the OS has chosen the caller as the next thread to lock it. Of all threads waiting to lock the mutex, only one may do so at a time. It is legal for the owning thread to lock an already-locked mutex. It must unlock it the same number of times before it is actually made available for other threads in the system (this is known as a "recursive mutex"). This function does not fail; if mutex is NULL, it will return immediately having locked nothing. If the mutex is valid, this function will always block until it can lock the mutex, and return with it locked. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_TryLockMutex @sa SDL_UnlockMutex<br>`mutex`：the mutex to lock. |
| <a id="api-7208daaf05f97303"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LockProperties(uint props)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48545) | Lock a group of properties. Obtain a multi-threaded lock for these properties. Other threads will wait while trying to lock these properties until they are unlocked. Properties must be unlocked before they are destroyed. The lock is automatically taken when setting individual properties, this function is only needed when you want to set several properties atomically or want to guarantee that properties being queried aren't freed in another thread. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_UnlockProperties<br>`props`：the properties to lock.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d84d327fd78fe481"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LockRwLockForReading(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRWLock* rwlock)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29731) | Lock the read/write lock for _read only_ operations. This will block until the rwlock is available, which is to say it is not locked for writing by any other thread. Of all threads waiting to lock the rwlock, all may do so at the same time as long as they are requesting read-only access; if a thread wants to lock for writing, only one may do so at a time, and no other threads, read-only or not, may hold the lock at the same time. It is legal for the owning thread to lock an already-locked rwlock for reading. It must unlock it the same number of times before it is actually made available for other threads in the system (this is known as a "recursive rwlock"). Note that locking for writing is not recursive (this is only available to read-only locks). It is illegal to request a read-only lock from a thread that already holds the write lock. Doing so results in undefined behavior. Unlock the write lock before requesting a read-only lock. (But, of course, if you have the write lock, you don't need further locks to read in any case.) This function does not fail; if rwlock is NULL, it will return immediately having locked nothing. If the rwlock is valid, this function will always block until it can lock the mutex, and return with it locked. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockRWLockForWriting @sa SDL_TryLockRWLockForReading @sa SDL_UnlockRWLock<br>`rwlock`：the read/write lock to lock. |
| <a id="api-8c79017fe2a1aadd"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LockRwLockForWriting(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRWLock* rwlock)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33316) | Lock the read/write lock for _write_ operations. This will block until the rwlock is available, which is to say it is not locked for reading or writing by any other thread. Only one thread may hold the lock when it requests write access; all other threads, whether they also want to write or only want read-only access, must wait until the writer thread has released the lock. It is illegal for the owning thread to lock an already-locked rwlock for writing (read-only may be locked recursively, writing can not). Doing so results in undefined behavior. It is illegal to request a write lock from a thread that already holds a read-only lock. Doing so results in undefined behavior. Unlock the read-only lock before requesting a write lock. This function does not fail; if rwlock is NULL, it will return immediately having locked nothing. If the rwlock is valid, this function will always block until it can lock the mutex, and return with it locked. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockRWLockForReading @sa SDL_TryLockRWLockForWriting @sa SDL_UnlockRWLock<br>`rwlock`：the read/write lock to lock. |
| <a id="api-8160091fe186506f"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LockSpinlock(int* @lock)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51784) | Lock a spin lock by setting it to a non-zero value. ***Please note that spinlocks are dangerous if you don't know what you're doing. Please be careful using any sort of spinlock!*** @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_TryLockSpinlock @sa SDL_UnlockSpinlock<br>`lock`：a pointer to a lock variable. |
| <a id="api-3ca86993ca5bbd4f"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.LockSurface(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34638) | Set up a surface for directly accessing the pixels. Between calls to SDL_LockSurface() / SDL_UnlockSurface(), you can write to and read from `surface->pixels`, using the pixel format stored in `surface->format`. Once you are done accessing the surface, you should use SDL_UnlockSurface() to release it. Not all surfaces require locking.
